Abstract: Trajectory tracking performance of Uncrewed Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) degrades during high-speed and agile flight due to the depletion of the battery and subsequent loss of maximum available thrust. In applications such as drone racing, the consequent trajectory tracking error leads to a collision with obstacles and a subsequent failure to complete the race. In this paper, we present a novel method for integrating battery and propulsion system models into a Nonlinear Model Predictive Controller (NMPC) framework to enable real-time prediction of the voltage, consumed current, power, and maximum available thrust of the platform. This enables our approach to account for the dynamic variations in the maximum available thrust of the UAV caused by battery discharge, allowing it to plan for the depleting thrust and improve trajectory tracking performance. A trajectory planning algorithm is implemented to replan the trajectory in-flight based on evolving thrust limits. The accuracy of the proposed model is verified in real-world flight experiments, while the effectiveness of the replanning algorithm is evaluated in simulation. Compared to an uncompensated flight, our novel approach demonstrates achieves a collision-free flight to achieve a 6-fold decrease in tracking Root Mean Square Error (RMSE), a 46 % increase in flight distance, and a 100 % increase in flight time in an obstacle-ridden environment.

</details>


### [109] [Embodied GPT-5.1: Evidence of a World Model?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23899)
*Roberto Spinelli,Thiago C. Martins*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究探索了多模态大模型GPT-5.1在没有物理实体训练、无模拟环境训练、无传感器运动经验的情况下，仅通过低分辨率第一人称图像和离散动作集，成功控制了真实移动机器人完成导航和目标物体接触任务，展现出空间记忆、物理推理和动作序列执行能力，挑战了"物理身体是发展世界模型智能的必要前提"的传统观点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证大型多模态语言模型（GPT-5.1）是否能在完全没有实体经验、模拟训练或传感器运动经验的情况下，作为物理移动机器人的高层控制器，从而挑战认知科学和机器人学中关于物理身体是智能发展必要前提的传统观点。

Method: 使用GPT-5.1作为高层控制器，通过低分辨率第一人称图像和离散动作集，控制真实移动机器人完成导航和物体导向行为（定位并接触目标玩具），在多次试验中评估其表现。

Result: GPT-5.1展现出空间推理和物理理解能力：能维持物体离开视野后的短期空间记忆、推断自身运动的物理后果、执行连贯动作序列（如碰撞物体后倒退验证结果）。同时也存在效率低下、定位策略不精确、远距离干扰物误识别等局限。

Conclusion: GPT-5.1在无任何实体相关训练的情况下，展现出类似于世界模型的行为，这一发现挑战了"物理身体是发展物理理解智能的必要前提"的传统认知，值得进一步研究大语言模型中物理理解能力的涌现机制、边界和鲁棒性。

Abstract: This exploratory study examines whether a large multimodal language model, GPT-5.1, can serve as the high-level controller of a physical mobile robot despite having no prior embodiment, no training in simulated environments, and no exposure to sensorimotor experience. Using only low-resolution first-person images and a discrete action set, the model was tasked with navigation and object-directed behaviors such as locating and contacting a target toy. Across multiple trials, GPT-5.1 demonstrated emergent capabilities that suggest elements of spatial reasoning and physical understanding. These included maintaining short-term memory of object locations after they left the camera frame, inferring the physical consequences of its own movements, and executing coherent action sequences such as colliding with an object and reversing to visually verify the outcome. At the same time, the model displayed inefficiencies and perceptual limitations, including imprecise alignment strategies and occasional misidentification of distant distractors. Overall, the results indicate that GPT-5.1 exhibits signs of world-model-like behavior in an embodied setting, despite the absence of any embodiment-related training, a finding that challenges long-standing views in cognitive science and robotics which hold that a physical body is a necessary prerequisite for developing such forms of intelligence. The findings motivate deeper investigation into the emergence, limits, and robustness of physical understanding in large language models.

</details>


### [110] [LeapBot-WA: World-Anchor Action Models via Predictive Latent Alignments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23969)
*Pei Liu,Nan Zheng,Lang Zhang,Daojie Peng,Yanan Zhang,Feilong Kong,Mingyue Feng,Jiachao Liu,Yaonong Wang,Qifeng Chen,Jun Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LeapBot-WA提出了一种基于预测-潜在范式的新型世界动作模型，通过JEPA架构和ISAE模块实现无需像素级别视频生成的高效机器人控制，在LIBERO和RoboTwin 2.0上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型依赖像素级视频生成，浪费计算资源重建与任务无关的视觉细节，且策略易受视觉干扰物影响，亟需更高效的潜在空间世界建模方法。

Method: 提出Predictive-Latent范式，核心创新包括：(1) 将JEPA作为World-Anchor实现预测语义对齐；(2) ISAE将非高斯预测特征重塑为扩散友好的流形；(3) 非对称混合Transformer架构，训练时Anchor Diffusion Transformer引导动作扩散Transformer，推理时剪枝实现零开销。

Result: 在LIBERO上达到预测模型SOTA，在RoboTwin 2.0上匹配顶级生成式WAMs，无需大规模轨迹预训练，展现出色的零样本鲁棒性和真实世界迁移能力。

Conclusion: LeapBot-WA建立了一种高效、鲁棒的潜在中心范式，为可扩展的机器人控制提供了新路径，摆脱了像素级视频生成的瓶颈。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) have emerged as a powerful paradigm for embodied intelligence, yet the prevailing reliance on pixel-level video generation creates a fundamental bottleneck. Forcing models to reconstruct task-irrelevant visual details dissipates representational capacity and renders policies vulnerable to visual distractors. In this paper, we propose LeapBot-WA, which establishes a novel Predictive-Latent paradigm for WAMs by operationalizing the Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) as a World-Anchor. Departing from the traditional reliance on visual synthesis, LeapBot-WA shifts the core of world modeling to Predictive Semantic Alignment, extracting abstract physical dynamics directly within a latent foundation space. To bridge the modality gap between non-Gaussian predictive features and diffusion priors, we introduce the Isotropic Semantic Autoencoder (ISAE), which reshapes the anchor's latent space into a diffusion-friendly manifold to prevent off-manifold drift. Furthermore, we design an Asymmetric Mixture-of-Transformers (MoT) architecture. During training, an Anchor Diffusion Transformer acts as a privileged dynamics expert to guide the Action Diffusion Transformer; at inference, this heavy dynamics branch is pruned, enabling zero-overhead execution. LeapBot-WA achieves state-of-the-art performance among predictive models on LIBERO and matches top-tier generative WAMs on RoboTwin 2.0 without requiring large-scale trajectory pre-training. It further demonstrates superior zero-shot robustness to unseen environments and successful real-world transfer, establishing a highly efficient and robust latent-centric paradigm for scalable robotic control. Code: https://github.com/LeapWM/leapbot-wa.

</details>


### [111] [Co-planning of Flight Corridors and Communication Infrastructure for Urban Drone Logistics Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23989)
*Yingjie He,Yikang Wang,Zhenyu Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CR-CMAB框架，通过联合优化基站部署和无人机飞行走廊，在保证通信质量的同时最小化基础设施投资和飞行距离，实验证明该方法优于基线方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作将通信基础设施优化与无人机飞行路径独立优化，导致不必要的基站部署或过多飞行绕行，缺乏两者联合优化的方法。

Method: 提出CR-CMAB（信道互惠引导的组合多臂老虎机）框架，利用3D射线追踪构建高保真无线电地图，通过覆盖感知的CMAB搜索选择基站组合，并利用信道互惠动态扩展搜索空间。

Result: 在详细案例研究中，CR-CMAB在适中的计算时间内优于基线方法，获得更具战略性的基站位置和更短的飞行走廊。

Conclusion: 该研究为未来智慧城市中成本效益高且通信可靠的UAM部署提供了实用的规划视角。

Abstract: Reliable wireless connectivity is essential for urban air mobility (UAM) networks in dense urban environments. It is therefore imperative to carefully plan the supporting communication infrastructure for UAM flight corridors. Most existing works optimize communication infrastructure and UAV flight paths independently, often leading to unnecessary base station (BS) deployment or excessive flight detours. This paper studies the joint optimization of BS deployment and UAV flight corridors in complex urban environments, aiming to minimize both infrastructure investment and flight distance while satisfying communication quality constraints. We propose CR-CMAB, a channel reciprocity-guided combinatorial multi-armed bandit framework. The framework constructs high-fidelity radio maps using 3D ray tracing, selects BS combinations via coverage-aware CMAB search, and dynamically expands the search space by identifying promising BS locations through channel reciprocity. Experimental results from a detailed case study demonstrate that CR-CMAB outperforms baseline methods with moderate computational time, yielding more strategically positioned BSs and shorter flight corridors. This study offers a practical planning perspective for cost-effective and communication-reliable UAM deployment in future smart cities.

</details>


### [112] [FutureRTC: Real-Time Robot Execution with Anticipatory-Conditioned Action Chunking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24008)
*Hai Jiang,Yixian Zou,Binbin Liang,Boqian Liu,Fanman Meng,Shuaicheng Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FutureRTC是一个即插即用的适配框架，通过预测执行时刻的观测和状态来解决VLA策略异步执行中的预测-执行错位问题，无需修改底层策略，在仿真和真实环境中实现了更平滑的轨迹、更快的执行和更高的任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略在实时部署中需要异步执行，即当前动作块执行的同时计算后续动作块，这会导致预测与执行之间的错位，表现为动作块间的不连续性。现有方法要么只是浅层地平滑块边界，要么需要昂贵的策略优化，或者仅前向预测本体状态而忽略了关键的视觉观测。

Method: FutureRTC包含两个核心模块：1) 状态校正模块，补偿前向滚动与执行时刻实际本体状态之间的差异；2) 观测预测模块，利用机器人运动作为显式物理先验，通过运动感知的特征传输与重建来预测执行时刻的视觉表示。此外，还引入了策略一致性损失，使从预测上下文生成的动作块与VLA策略在预期执行时刻输入下生成的动作块对齐。

Result: 在仿真和真实环境中的大量实验表明，FutureRTC对推理延迟具有优越的鲁棒性，实现了更平滑的轨迹、更快的执行和持续更高的任务成功率。

Conclusion: FutureRTC作为一种即插即用的适配框架，有效解决了异步VLA控制中的预测-执行错位问题，在不修改底层策略的情况下提升了实时部署性能。

Abstract: Real-time deployment of Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies necessitates asynchronous execution, wherein subsequent action chunks are computed concurrently with the execution of the current chunk, leading to prediction-execution misalignment and manifesting as inter-chunk discontinuities. Existing methods either superficially smooth chunk boundaries, require costly policy optimization, or exclusively forward-predict proprioceptive states yet neglect critical visual observations. In this paper, we propose \textbf{FutureRTC}, a plug-and-play adaptation framework that predicts execution-time observations and states for asynchronous VLA control without modifying the underlying policy. Specifically, FutureRTC features a state correction module to compensate for the discrepancy between rolled-forward and actual execution-time proprioceptive states and an observation prediction module that forecasts execution-time visual representations by leveraging robot motion as an explicit physical prior through motion-aware feature transport and reconstruction. Furthermore, we introduce a policy consistency loss to align the action chunks generated from predicted contexts with those produced under the expected execution-time inputs of the VLA policy. Extensive experiments across simulated and real-world environments demonstrate that FutureRTC achieves superior robustness to inference delays, resulting in smoother trajectories, faster execution, and consistently higher task success rates.

</details>


### [113] [Moving-Horizon Estimation and Nonlinear Model Predictive Control of Cable-Driven Soft Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24029)
*Lingxiao Xun,Haihong Li,Gang Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于简化Cosserat动力学模型的移动视界估计与非线性模型预测控制框架，用于线驱动软体机械臂的精确控制，通过平滑的线长驱动建模和无需直接力传感的估计方法，实现了实时末端执行器位置跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体机械臂的精确控制面临挑战，主要困难在于开发既准确又计算可行的模型用于基于模型的估计和控制。

Method: 基于简化Cosserat动力学，提出MHE与NMPC框架；通过近似缆绳张力与松弛的互补关系开发平滑的线长驱动模型；利用MHE从末端执行器位姿测量和线长信息估计状态；采用NMPC实现任务空间控制并满足线长与线速率约束。

Result: 仿真验证了估计器和控制器在多缆软体机械臂上的有效性；四缆原型实验表明该框架可实时实现，并实现精确的末端执行器位置跟踪。

Conclusion: 基于简化Cosserat动力学的MHE-NMPC框架能够有效实现线驱动软体机械臂的实时精确控制，无需直接张力传感。

Abstract: Precise control of soft manipulators remains challenging due to the difficulty of developing accurate yet computationally tractable models for model-based estimation and control. Reduced Cosserat-rod models provide a physics-based and control-oriented description of soft-robot dynamics, offering an explicit alternative to purely data-driven input-output representations. In this paper, we propose a moving-horizon estimation (MHE) and nonlinear model predictive control (NMPC) framework for cable-driven soft manipulators based on reduced Cosserat dynamics. A smooth cable-length-driven modeling formulation is developed by approximating the complementarity relationship between cable tension and cable slackness, enabling cable-length control without direct tension sensing. Based on this formulation, an MHE method is introduced to estimate the reduced state and reconstruct the manipulator configuration from end-effector pose measurements and cable-length information. An NMPC controller is then formulated to achieve task-space control under cable-length and cable-rate constraints. The proposed framework is validated through numerical simulations and experiments. Simulation results demonstrate the effectiveness of the estimator and controller for pose and strain-related regulation on a multi-cable soft manipulator. Experimental results on a four-cable prototype further show that the proposed MHE-NMPC scheme can be implemented in real time and enables accurate end-effector position tracking through cable-length control.

</details>


### [114] [WARL: Wrench-Augmented Reinforcement Learning for Task-Agnostic Learning in Legged Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24036)
*Keita Yoneda,Kento Kawaharazuka,Kei Okada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Wrench-Augmented Reinforcement Learning (WARL)方法，通过在动作空间中引入力/力矩（wrench）来增强强化学习的探索能力，并配合基于成功率的课程机制逐步消除wrench，最终实现纯关节控制下的四足机器人稳健运动学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于关节空间的强化学习在足式机器人运动控制中探索能力受限，难以高效学习复杂行为。

Method: 提出WARL方法，在动作空间中引入wrench（力和力矩）以增强探索；配合成功率驱动的课程机制（Switching Curriculum）逐步消除wrench，使策略最终回归纯关节控制。

Result: 在四足机器人实验中，WARL能够在多种地形和运动任务中稳健学习，无需针对特定地形调整奖励或设计复杂课程。消融实验验证了Switching Curriculum的有效性。

Conclusion: 基于wrench的探索增强能有效提升学习效率，但设计时需与机器人物理结构保持一致，这是未来的关键挑战。

Abstract: While reinforcement learning for legged robots has achieved high motor performance, it has been constrained by the limited exploration capability of actions confined to the joint space. To address this issue, this study proposes a new method, Wrench-Augmented Reinforcement Learning (WARL), which introduces a wrenche (force and torque) into the action space. The proposed method combines wrench-guided exploration with a success rate-based curriculum mechanism to expand exploration capabilities in the early stages of learning, with the ultimate goal of acquiring behaviors based solely on joint control. Experiments using a quadruped robot demonstrated that WARL can learn robustly across diverse terrains and motor tasks without requiring terrain-specific reward adjustments or complex curriculum designs. Furthermore, an ablation study verified the effectiveness of the Switching Curriculum, which gradually eliminates the wrench. On the other hand, we also show that introducing a wrench can encourage behaviors that do not sufficiently exploit the robot's physical embodiment. These findings suggest that while wrench-based exploration enhancement is effective for improving learning efficiency, designing it in a way that is consistent with the robot's physical structure is a critical future challenge.

</details>


### [115] [Effective Parameters, Real Behavior: Renormalization for Robotics -- From Infinite Electron Mass to Sim-to-Real Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24079)
*Youran Sun,Jiaxuan Guo,Xingyu Ren,Chugang Yi,Haizhao Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出使用重正化方法，通过有效参数（而非更精确的模拟器）来弥合机器人的仿真-现实差距，并展示了在PD控制、绳索操作和水下游泳中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的弥合仿真-现实差距的方法是构建越来越精确的模拟器，但这种方法存在局限性。作者希望探索一种互补路径：通过重正化思想，使用与分辨率相关的有效参数来吸收模拟器忽略的细节，从而在不需要完全精确模拟的情况下再现真实行为。

Method: 1. 在有限仿真频率的PD控制中，通过解析推导证明比例反馈改变有效微分增益，微分反馈改变有效惯性。2. 将这一视角扩展到动态绳索操作和水下游泳等复杂场景。3. 提出一套实用流程：选择可观测量、识别被忽略的物理因素、确定有效参数。

Result: 在PD控制中成功展示了重正化机制：有效参数与实测物理值不同，因为它们补偿了模拟器省略的细节。该视角可推广到绳索操作和水下游泳等更复杂的机器人任务。

Conclusion: 重正化为机器人学提供了一条弥合仿真-现实差距的互补路径：使用有效参数来再现真实行为，而不是追求完全精确的模拟。

Abstract: Bridging the sim-to-real gap is a central problem in robotics, and the prevailing approach is to build increasingly accurate simulators. Here, we propose another approach based on renormalization: using effective, resolution-dependent parameters to absorb details omitted by the simulator and reproduce real behavior. These parameters may differ from measured physical values because they compensate for what the simulator leaves out. We demonstrate this mechanism analytically for proportional--derivative (PD) control at finite simulation frequency, where proportional feedback changes the effective derivative gain and derivative feedback changes the effective inertia. We then interpret dynamic rope manipulation and underwater swimming through the same perspective. Finally, we present a practical procedure for choosing observables, identifying omitted physics, and determining effective parameters. Renormalization offers robotics a complementary path across the sim-to-real gap: effective parameters, real behavior.

</details>


### [116] [A Case Study on the Acceptance of a Humanoid Robotic Head Employed in Three Public Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24113)
*Marcel Heisler,Luca Randecker,Christian Becker-Asano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了在三个不同公共场所（旅游信息中心、市图书馆和建筑管理局）连续运行多天的交互式机器人头部。该机器人能处理自然语言并通过情感模拟后端生成带有情绪表达的多模态语言回应。TAM2问卷结果显示，用户普遍认为机器人有用且易用，但旅游信息中心和图书馆比建筑管理局更适合。多语言回应受到赞赏，但20%的用户认为响应速度过慢影响了对话流畅性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究表明类人机器人的接受度高度依赖其运行环境和执行相关任务的能力。本文旨在探索不同公共场所对交互式机器人接受度的影响，以及多语言自然语言处理和情感表达在真实场景中的表现。

Method: 机器人通过自然语言处理生成结合情感模拟的多模态语言回应。在三个不同的公共场所（旅游信息中心、市图书馆、建筑管理局）连续运行数天，邀请访客用自己的语言与机器人对话，并使用TAM2问卷评估用户接受度。

Result: 用户普遍认为机器人有用且易用，但不同场所存在差异：旅游信息中心和图书馆比建筑管理局更适合，后者用户交互意愿较低。多语言回应受到高度赞赏，但约20%的用户认为响应时间过慢影响对话流畅性。

Conclusion: 交互式机器人头部在旅游信息中心和图书馆等公共场所的接受度优于办公室环境（如建筑管理局）。多语言功能和情感表达是亮点，但响应速度仍需改进以提升对话流畅性。

Abstract: Previous research has shown that a human-like robot's acceptance heavily depends on the setting in which it operates and its ability to perform relevant tasks. This paper, first, reports on how our robot processes natural language to generate a multimodal, verbal response integrating emotional expressions based on an emotion simulation backend. Then, it describes how visitors were invited to speak with our robot in their own language at three different, public locations, where the robot was running continuously for several days. The TAM2 questionnaire results reveal that on average users were motivated to use the robot and found it rather useful and easy to use regardless of the specific location. However, public spaces like the tourist information and the city library seem to be a better fit for our interactive, robotic head than an office environment such as the building authority, where the willingness to interact was lower. Overall, the robot's multi-lingual responses were very much appreciated, but every fifth user found the response time too slow impeding the dialog flow, which remains to be improved in future work.

</details>


### [117] [DeVA: Decoupled Video-Action Model with physical guidance for robot policy learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24159)
*Mengqi Zhang,Sahil Khose,Simar Kareer,Yuchen Song,Unnat Jain,Judy Hoffman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DeVA提出了一种解耦的视频-动作模型，通过分离视频和动作专家、多层级特征传输以及物理显著性引导（如 affordance/深度），在有限数据下实现了更快的收敛和更强的机器人操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作模型主要基于静态预训练，缺乏对物理动态和时间因果的监督，控制相关知识需从下游机器人演示中学习；而现有视频-动作模型要么将视频与动作预测耦合在共享骨干中导致策略优化困难，要么未充分利用视频信息来指导动作分支。

Method: 提出DeVA（解耦视频-动作模型），包含专门的视频专家和动作专家，通过多层级特征传输实现信息交换，并利用物理显著性引导（affordance/深度）对中间视频特征和动作流进行监督。

Result: 在仿真基准和真实世界部署中，DeVA在有限数据下表现强劲，相比统一架构收敛更快，且物理引导带来了明显的性能提升。

Conclusion: 解耦的视频-动作架构结合多层级特征传输与物理显著性引导，能够更高效地利用视频先验知识，提升机器人策略学习的泛化能力和数据效率。

Abstract: Generalizable robot manipulation requires policies that can anticipate how visual scenes evolve while executing language instructions. While recent Vision-Language-Action models benefit from large-scale pretraining, their predominantly static pretraining objectives provide limited supervision for physical dynamics and temporal causality, leaving control-relevant knowledge to be learned from downstream robot demonstrations. Video generative models offer a promising foundation by encoding rich spatiotemporal priors through future predictions. However, existing Video-Action Models either couple video and action prediction in a shared backbone, making policy adaptation harder to optimize, or under-utilize video information when guiding the action branch. In this work, we introduce DeVA, a Decoupled Video-Action model with specialized video and action experts, multi-level feature transfer, and physically salient guidance. DeVA transfers representations from multiple video layers to the action expert, enabling rich information exchange while making policy learning more tractable. It further supervises intermediate video features and the action stream with physically salient guidance (affordance/depth). Experiments on both simulation benchmarks and real-world deployment demonstrate strong performance with limited data, faster convergence than a unified architecture, and clear performance gains from physical guidance.

</details>


### [118] [Not Forgotten: Implementation and Evaluation of a Personalized Episodic Memory for the Humanoid Robot Head Kim](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24190)
*Steve Aschenbrenner,Marcel Heisler,Thomas Sievers,Christian Becker-Asano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种轻量级情景记忆模块，结合向量语义检索与LLM控制对话系统，部署于人形机器人Kim上。通过混合打分函数（余弦相似度+记忆强度）检索历史交互并注入生成提示。在线视频研究（N=43）表明，情景记忆显著提升感知社交性（d=0.60）、可信赖度（d=0.62）和温暖感（d=0.56），且未引发隐私不适或恐怖谷效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前依赖大语言模型的社交机器人无法跨会话保留信息，违背社交期望，阻碍持久关系形成。

Method: 提出轻量级情景记忆模块，采用混合打分函数（余弦相似度+记忆强度度量）进行向量语义检索，将相关历史交互注入LLM对话系统的生成提示中，部署于人形机器人头部Kim。

Result: 在线视频研究（N=43）显示：情景记忆显著提升感知社交性（d=0.60, p<.001），最强效应为可信赖度（d=0.62）和温暖感（d=0.56）；感知干扰无变化（d=0.00），未触发隐私不适或恐怖谷效应。

Conclusion: 情景记忆在人机交互中充当社交润滑剂，提升关系质量而不引发负面情感反应。

Abstract: Social robots that rely on large language models for conversation are unable to retain information across sessions. This absence of memory violates social expectations, potentially preventing the formation of persistent relationships. This paper presents a lightweight episodic memory module that integrates vector-based semantic retrieval with an LLM-controlled dialog system, deployed on the humanoid robot head Kim. The module employs a hybrid scoring function combining cosine similarity with a memory strength metric to retrieve contextually relevant past interactions and inject them into the generation prompt. The system was evaluated in a within-subjects video-based online study (N = 43) using the Human-Robot Interaction Evaluation Scale (HRIES). Results show that episodic memory significantly increased perceived sociability (d = 0.60, p < .001), with the strongest effects on perceived trustworthiness (d = 0.62) and warmth (d = 0.56). Perceived disturbance remained unchanged (d = 0.00), indicating that the implemented approach to personalized recall did not trigger privacy-related discomfort or uncanny valley effects. These findings suggest that episodic memory serves as a social lubricant in embodied Human-Robot Interaction, enhancing relational quality without eliciting negative affective responses.

</details>


### [119] [Surgical Re-enactment for Operating Room Workflow Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24206)
*Jana Nina Friedrich,Andrea Karin Maria Ross,Angelo Henriques,Mario Peter Martin Weisser,Ling Zhang,Mohammad Ali Nasseri*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在重构手术室中重演完整手术流程的方法，用于生成可重复、可标注的手术工作流数据集，以支持手术机器人等新技术在智能手术室中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在真实手术室中采集包含所有人员动作的多视角数据存在伦理审查、空间限制和无菌规定等巨大挑战，因此需要一种替代方案来生成高质量的工作流数据集。

Method: 结合专家咨询、结构化工作流形式化、手术室重构、角色扮演训练、真实手术室观察、迭代录制及事后复盘，形成一套完整的方法论。

Result: 成功开发出针对机器人辅助眼科手术的可重复、可标注的工作流数据集，支持活动识别模型训练、场景图生成和手术过程模型形式化。

Conclusion: 该方法可被其他研究团队无缝迁移至各自的手术领域，为智能手术室的建设提供数据基础。

Abstract: The introduction of new technologies, such as surgical robots, is driving the vision of a connected, smart operating room (OR). However, realizing this vision requires a deep understanding of surgical workflows, which relies on realistic datasets capturing the actions of all OR personnel from both full room and surgical field perspectives. Acquiring such data in real ORs is prohibitively challenging due to factors such as ethics committee approvals, limited space for camera installation, and sterility regulations preventing the use of tracking markers. We present a step-by-step methodology for re-enacting complete surgical procedures in a reconstructed OR. This approach enables the creation of repeatable and annotatable workflow datasets for training activity recognition models, generating scene graphs, and formalizing surgical process models. Developed for robot-assisted ophthalmic surgery, our methodology combines expert consultation, structured workflow formalization, OR reconstruction, role-based training, real OR observation, and iterative recording with post-take debriefing. We provide concrete recommendations to allow other research groups to seamlessly adopt this methodology for their own surgical domains.

</details>


### [120] [FloAff-Kitchen: Bridging Navigation and Manipulation via Canonical and Progressive Floor Affordance Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24207)
*Ping Zhong,Manling Teng,Tao Wu,Bolei Chen,Jiazhi Xia,Jianxin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一框架，通过规范表示学习和渐进式先验学习，解决移动操作机器人中地板可通行性预测的表示歧义和技能异构问题，并在新建立的跨场景多视角基准上取得最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在预测地板可通行性时存在两方面问题：一是无关空间上下文和物体任意朝向导致的表示歧义；二是不同操作技能之间的共享知识和任务特定知识纠缠不清，影响泛化能力。

Method: 提出统一框架：(1) 规范地板可通行性表示(CFAR)，通过保留局部结构、消除与机器人基座放置无关的空间变化来学习规范交互几何；(2) 渐进式地板可通行性学习(PFAL)，从基础操作任务学习可迁移的先验知识，并逐步适应异构下游操作技能。

Result: 建立了首个跨场景、多视角的FloAff-Kitchen基准，涵盖多种操作技能、场景布局、家具风格和视角。在三个基准设置上的大量实验表明该方法始终优于强基线，消融研究验证了各组件的贡献。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决了地板可通行性预测中的表示歧义和技能异构问题，为移动操作机器人提供了更优的基座放置决策。

Abstract: Mobile manipulation requires robots to identify Floor Affordance (FloAff) that maximizes downstream manipulation success rather than merely ensuring navigation feasibility. FloAff prediction is a target-conditioned local spatial reasoning problem, yet existing methods suffer from representation ambiguity caused by irrelevant spatial context and arbitrary object orientations, while entangling shared and task-specific knowledge across heterogeneous manipulation skills. To address these challenges, we propose a unified framework for FloAff prediction from egocentric multimodal perception, consisting of canonical representation learning and progressive affordance prior learning. Specifically, we introduce a Canonical Floor Affordance Representation (CFAR), which learns canonical interaction geometry by preserving affordance-relevant local structure while eliminating nuisance spatial variations unrelated to robot base placement. We further propose Progressive Floor Affordance Learning (PFAL), which learns transferable FloAff priors from a foundation manipulation task and progressively adapts them to heterogeneous downstream manipulation skills. To facilitate systematic evaluation, we establish the first cross-scene, multi-view FloAff-Kitchen benchmark covering diverse manipulation skills, scene layouts, furniture styles, and viewpoints. Extensive experiments on three benchmark settings demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms strong baselines, while ablation studies validate the contribution of each proposed component. Project page: https://csu-hero-lab.github.io/FloAff-Kitchen_Web/

</details>


### [121] [Quality-Adaptive Multi-UAV 3D Reconstruction with Sparse Workload Redistribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24233)
*Benjamin Sportich,Kenza Boubakri,Olivier Simonin,Alessandro Renzaglia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种质量自适应的多无人机协同3D重建策略，通过TSDF置信度引导视角生成和信息增益估计，结合局部分散惩罚因子与全局不平衡校正机制，在提升路径效率的同时实现高保真度的三维地图重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前空中平台在计算和能量方面受限，多无人机协同在线规划面临挑战，缺乏有效的协调策略来构建高质量3D地图。

Method: 提出质量自适应的去中心化决策策略：1）基于TSDF置信度的质量导向标准用于视角生成与信息增益估计；2）两级协调机制：视角评估中引入惩罚因子促进局部分散，以及基于正则化聚类和最优任务分配的全局不平衡校正。

Result: 仿真结果表明，该方法相比现有先进多无人机探索方法，路径效率更高，同时在覆盖率和精度方面实现了更高保真度的重建。

Conclusion: 提出的质量自适应去中心化策略能有效解决多无人机在线协调难题，兼顾路径效率与重建质量，代码已开源。

Abstract: 3D reconstruction of unknown environments is a key application in robotics but is severely limited by the computational and energy capabilities of current aerial platforms. Deploying multiple UAVs and providing efficient and scalable path planning strategies are common approaches, but effective online coordination among UAVs remains a significant challenge. To address this problem, we propose a quality-adaptive decentralized decision-making strategy to build a 3D map with user-defined degrees of fidelity. The approach integrates a quality-oriented criterion based on TSDF confidence into view generation and information gain estimation to produce viewpoints consistent with the desired fidelity target. Additionally, we employ two levels of coordination: a penalty factor in the viewpoint evaluation to encourage local dispersion among the UAVs and a global imbalance correction mechanism. The latter, based on regularized clustering and optimal task assignment, is only triggered when an unbalanced configuration relative to high-information regions is detected. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed method improves path efficiency compared to state-of-the-art multi-UAV exploration approaches, while also achieving higher-fidelity reconstructions in terms of coverage and accuracy. We make our code publicly available to the community.

</details>


### [122] [FeelWorld: Visuo-Tactile World Model for Hierarchical Contact Prediction and Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24267)
*Wenxuan Ma,Chaofan Zhang,Chao Xue,Yinghao Cai,Guocai Yao,Shaowei Cui,Shuo Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FeelWorld，一种层次化视触觉世界模型，通过联合预测视觉潜在特征和三种触觉状态（接触状态、3D触觉潜变量、滑移状态），并引入接触门控非对称注意力机制，在接触前保持纯视觉预测、接触时启用视触觉联合预测，显著提升了接触丰富场景下的未来预测准确性，零样本规划成功率达81.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉世界模型主要捕捉外观动态，忽略了接触丰富交互中的触觉状态，导致生成的未来预测在视觉上看似合理但违反物理动力学。

Method: 提出层次化视触觉世界模型FeelWorld，包含：（1）联合预测视觉潜在特征和三种触觉状态（接触状态、3D触觉潜变量、滑移状态）的共享潜在动力学模型；（2）接触门控非对称注意力机制，接触前保持纯视觉预测通路，接触时启用视触觉联合预测；（3）自回归展开训练与上下文噪声注入增强鲁棒性；（4）基于预测接触和滑移状态的接触感知CEM规划。

Result: 在芯片抓取、水果抓取和USB插入任务上，FeelWorld将10步LPIPS从0.084降至0.058，80步自回归展开后LPIPS比视觉基线低61%；零样本规划成功率达81.7%。

Conclusion: FeelWorld有效将触觉传感融入世界模型，在接触丰富操作任务中显著提升未来预测准确性及规划性能。

Abstract: Humans plan physical interactions by imagining the possible outcomes of candidate actions. However, existing visual world models primarily capture appearance dynamics while overlooking the tactile states that govern contact-rich interactions, potentially producing imagined futures that appear visually plausible but violate physical dynamics. We introduce FeelWorld, a hierarchical visuo-tactile world model that jointly predicts future visual latents and three tactile states. FeelWorld organizes these states hierarchically as contact state, a 3D tactile latent that encodes force-related information, and slip state. These states are jointly predicted by a shared latent dynamics model with explicit supervision. To prevent irrelevant tactile signals during free-space motion from degrading visual prediction, we introduce a contact-gated asymmetric attention mechanism that maintains a visual-only prediction pathway before contact and enables joint visuo-tactile dynamics prediction during contact. The model is further trained with autoregressive rollouts and context noise injection to improve robustness to compounding errors. The predicted contact and slip states also support contact-aware CEM planning. Experiments on chip grasping, fruit grasping, and USB insertion show that FeelWorld reduces 10-step LPIPS from 0.084 to 0.058 and maintains an LPIPS that is 61% lower than that of the visual baseline after an 80-step autoregressive rollout. FeelWorld also achieves an average zero-shot planning success rate of 81.7%, providing an effective approach for incorporating tactile sensing into world models.

</details>


### [123] [Learning Adaptive Multi-Task Guidance, Navigation, and Control via Hypernetworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24292)
*Ricard Marsal I Castan,Aman Arora,Antoine Richard,Andrej Orsula,Cédric Pradalier,Miguel A. Olivares-Méndez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出HYPER-GNC多任务强化学习框架，通过超网络将物理感知任务嵌入映射到共享策略权重，使单个控制器能同时完成速度跟踪、对接、检测和障碍物规避导航四种GNC任务，并在物理卫星模拟器上验证了仿真到现实的迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轨道环境中自主自由飞行机器人需要兼具通用性与资源效率的控制器，但为每个任务配置单独策略在架构上存在脆弱性，且限制了操作灵活性，因此亟需一种能高效处理多种GNC任务的统一框架。

Method: 采用多任务强化学习框架HYPER-GNC，利用超网络将物理感知任务嵌入映射到共享actor-critic策略的权重，通过连续嵌入空间实现对新任务配置的零样本泛化。

Result: HYPER-GNC在样本效率上与单任务专家相当，在显著惯性扰动和外力作用下保持稳定性，并在物理卫星模拟器上成功跨越仿真到现实的鸿沟，所有任务配置均得到验证。

Conclusion: HYPER-GNC通过超网络实现单个紧凑控制器掌握多种GNC任务，具备出色的样本效率和鲁棒性，且能零样本泛化到新任务配置，代码和模型已公开以支持可复现性。

Abstract: Autonomous free-flying robots in orbital environments require controllers that are both versatile and resource-efficient, yet maintaining a separate, task-specific policy for each mission profile is architecturally brittle and limits operational flexibility as requirements evolve. We introduce HYPER-GNC, a multi-task reinforcement learning framework in which a hypernetwork maps physics-informed task embeddings to the weights of a shared actor-critic policy, enabling a single compact controller to master four distinct GNC tasks: velocity tracking, docking, inspection, and navigation with obstacle avoidance. The continuous embedding space allows the controller to generalize to novel mission configurations at deployment time without any retraining. Extensive experiments demonstrate that HYPER-GNC achieves sample efficiency comparable to single-task specialists while maintaining stability under significant inertial perturbations and external body wrenches. We further validate the framework on a physical satellite emulator, successfully bridging the simulation-to-reality gap across all mission profiles. Code, trained models, and deployment scripts are made publicly available to support reproducibility.

</details>


### [124] [PAC-DP: PAC-Bayesian Diffusion Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24296)
*Mohammad Hasan Yeganegi,Dian Yu,Andrea Del Prete,Majid Khadiv,Matteo Saveriano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PAC-DP，通过将扩散策略建模为贝叶斯神经网络并引入PAC-Bayes泛化界约束，在标准去噪损失上增加KL散度正则项，提升了机器人操作任务中扩散策略的性能，尤其在低数据场景下效果显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的扩散策略训练仅最小化去噪目标，在机器人领域有限数据条件下对泛化能力的控制不足。

Method: 将扩散策略建模为贝叶斯神经网络，定义PAC-Bayes泛化界，在标准去噪损失基础上增加后验与先验参数分布的KL散度正则项。

Result: 改进的去噪性能、更低的变分负对数似然、更高的操作任务成功率，尤其在低数据训练场景和复杂任务中提升最为明显。

Conclusion: PAC-DP为机器人策略学习提供了一个理论上有依据的框架，在不显著增加训练时间的情况下实现了更优的泛化性能。

Abstract: Diffusion Policies (DPs) are able to perform complex manipulation tasks. However, DPs are typically trained by minimizing a denoising objective, which provides limited control over generalization in the finite-data regimes common in robotics. In this letter, we propose PAC-DP, an approach that increases the performance of DPs in robotic manipulation tasks. By modeling the DP as a Bayesian neural network, and defining a PAC-Bayes generalization bound, we derive a novel training objective that augments the standard denoising loss with a Kullback-Leibler divergence regularizer between the posterior and prior parameter distributions. From the theoretical perspective, our approach provides a principled approach to regularize the training of DPs without significantly increasing the training time. From the practical point of view, experimental results demonstrate improved denoising performance, lower variational negative log-likelihood, and higher success rates across multiple robotic manipulation benchmarks. Crucially, the largest improvements are observed in low-data training regimes and complex tasks, establishing PAC-DP as a theoretically grounded framework for robot policy learning.

</details>


### [125] [A note on the motion representation and configuration update in time stepping schemes for the constrained rigid body](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24317)
*A. Müller*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文从几何角度分析约束刚体动力学建模，证明SE(3)作为构型空间可确保数值积分精确满足约束，而SO(3)×R³则不能，结论是SE(3)更适合约束刚体DAE建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多体动力学中构型空间选择（SO(3)×R³ vs SE(3)）对约束满足精度的影响这一长期被观察但缺乏理论解释的问题。

Method: 采用几何视角，分析不同Lie群作为构型空间时约束满足的条件，证明约束定义子群时数值积分可精确满足约束。

Result: 只有当约束定义构型空间的子群时，数值格式才能精确满足约束；SE(3)是约束刚体DAE建模的合适构型空间。

Conclusion: SE(3)是约束刚体数值DAE建模的正确构型空间，但该结论不能直接推广到多体系统。

Abstract: The dynamics of a holonomically constrained rigid body can be modeled by Newton-Euler equations subjected to geometric constraints. This is frequently formulated as a differential-algebraic equation (DAE) system of index 1. Inmultibody system (MBS) dynamics it is common (1) to numerically solve this system by means of integration schemes for ordinary differential equations, and (2) to treat the rigid body motion on the direct product Lie group SO (3)R3, although rigid body motions form the semidirect product Lie group SE (3). It is has been observed that the constraint satisfaction depends on which Lie group is used as configuration space (c-space). In this paper the problem is considered from a geometric perspective. It is shown that the constraints are exactly satisfied by a numerical integration scheme if they define a subgroup of the c-space. The subgroups of SE (3) have a significance for modeling mechanical systems, including lower kinematic (Reuleaux) pairs and are implicitly used in MBS modeling. It is concluded that SE (3) is the appropriate cspace for numerical DAE modeling of a constrained rigid body. This result does not immediately apply to MBS, however.

</details>


### [126] [Continual-RL for Generalization in Autonomous Racing on the RoboRacer Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24320)
*Joel Siegert,Edoardo Ghignone,Michele Magno*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出基于持续反向传播的持续强化学习框架，仅用真实数据训练通用策略，并在15分钟内微调使其在自动驾驶竞速任务中超越传统控制器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人需要在不断变化的环境中适应，而仿真无法覆盖所有真实情况，物理世界中的强化学习成为必要。当前持续强化学习的框架和方法仍探索不足。

Method: 提出基于持续反向传播的持续强化学习框架，利用真实世界数据训练通用策略，并在新场景中快速微调；同时提出离线RL对比方法，并进行可塑性仿真分析。

Result: 仅用真实数据训练的通用策略可在15分钟微调内超越传统控制器性能。

Conclusion: 持续RL框架能有效解决真实场景下的快速适应问题，为自动驾驶竞速等持续学习任务提供了可行方案。

Abstract: A key challenge in modern robotics is to adapt to changing environments, a challenge that is exacerbated when simulations cannot encompass every possible real-world configuration, and therefore Reinforcement Learning (RL) in the physical world becomes necessary. Continual Reinforcement Learning provides the tools to address this challenge; however, both the frameworks and the methods remain underexplored. Autonomous Racing and in particular the RoboRacer competition provide a testing ground for such methods, as learning to drive on a new track-floor combination with the least amount of new experience naturally frames a continual learning problem. This work tries to address this gap by proposing a continual RL framework based on Continual Backpropagation that is able, with only real-world data, to train a generalistic policy on a set of tracks and then fine- tune it within 15 minutes to outperform classical controllers. Furthermore, a comparison method based on offline RL is proposed, and a simulation analysis of the plasticity properties of the methods is conducted.

</details>


### [127] [Model Predictive Planner for UAV Navigation in Non-Convex Air Corridors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24369)
*Henrique Silva,Marcelo A. Santos,Guilherme V. Raffo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于混合整数跟踪模型预测控制的无人机运动规划框架，能够在非凸城市空中走廊中生成动态有效且满足约束的轨迹，无需依赖外部全局规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决无人机在非凸城市空中走廊导航中因几何非凸性导致的局部极小值问题，以及现有方法依赖外部全局规划阶段的局限性。

Method: 采用混合整数跟踪模型预测控制（MI-TMPC）框架，将走廊可行性和动态一致性约束整合为单一优化问题，并嵌入基于最短路径的偏移成本与可行性约束以规避局部极小值。

Result: 数值仿真表明该方法生成的轨迹动态有效、满足走廊约束并能收敛到目标点，且无需外部全局规划。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架为无人机在非凸城市空中走廊中的自主导航提供了一种有效的单阶段规划方案。

Abstract: This work presents a motion planning framework for UAV navigation in non-convex urban air corridors. The planner is based on a mixed-integer tracking model predictive control formulation that enforces corridor feasibility and dynamic consistency within a single optimization problem. To guarantee convergence to the target and mitigate the occurrence of local minima induced by non-convex geometry, a shortest-path-based offset cost with feasibility constraints is embedded directly into the planning problem. Numerical simulations show that the proposed formulation generates dynamically valid trajectories that satisfy the corridor constraints and converge to the target without relying on external global planning stages.

</details>


### [128] [ArmnetBench v0.1: Parallel Real-World Evaluation of Manipulation Policies on a Low-Cost Arm Farm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24481)
*Praveen Selvaraj,Lorenzo Uttini,Ville Kuosmanen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ArmnetBench v0.1是一个基于低成本SO-101机械臂集群的机器人操作策略评估基准，包含2,518次策略部署和600次参考演示，所有3,118个轨迹均带三分类标签（成功/次优/失败），支持下游学习任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界评估是发展通用机器人操作策略的瓶颈，每次部署都需要物理硬件和操作员进行设置、重置和评分，成本高昂且效率低下。

Method: 基于低成本SO-101机械臂集群（arm farm），在轻度现场监督下运行，对比7种策略在12个任务上的表现（含单臂和双臂配置），每种策略每个任务使用50个演示进行训练/微调。

Result: 包含2,518次策略部署和600个参考演示，共3,118个带三分类标签的轨迹，建立了初始排行榜。

Conclusion: 该基准验证了低成本机械臂集群用于机器人策略评估的可行性，带质量标签的轨迹可用于奖励建模、预测世界模型和混合质量数据策略训练等下游学习任务，数据集已以LeRobot v3.0和RoboMeter格式发布。

Abstract: Real-world evaluation is a bottleneck in developing generalist robot manipulation policies. Each rollout requires physical hardware and an operator to set up, reset, and score it. We introduce ArmnetBench v0.1, a benchmark run on a fleet of low-cost SO-101 cells under light on-site supervision. v0.1 validates this arm farm end to end and compares 7 policies across 12 tasks with both single-arm and bimanual configurations. Each policy is trained or fine-tuned on 50 demonstrations per task; the benchmark contains 2,518 policy rollouts and 600 reference demonstrations. All 3,118 episodes carry a three-way label (successful, suboptimal, or failure). Policy rollouts are human-scored, while demonstrations are successful by construction. Beyond evaluation, its quality-labelled trajectories support downstream learning, from reward and predictive world models to policies trained on mixed-quality data. The leaderboard is an initial comparison under this shared budget. We release the 3,118 core episodes in LeRobot v3.0 and RoboMeter formats.

</details>


### [129] [Distributed Coordination for Resilient Multi-UAV Remote Sensing: A Photovoltaic Inspection Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24482)
*Guillermo GP-Lenza,Miguel Fernandez-Cortizas,Martin Molina,Pascual Campoy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SwarmLink通信架构，通过分布式拍卖机制实现无人机群在遥感任务中的自主协调，无需地面站介入即可处理目标分配、平台故障和数据质量问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集中式地面站是单点故障点，链路中断或站故障会导致传感缺口且需操作员干预，无法充分发挥多无人机协同的潜力。

Method: 设计SwarmLink对等通信基础设施，非侵入式扩展现有航空框架；基于分布式拍卖机制统一处理初始目标分配、平台故障恢复和数据质量触发的重分配。

Result: 三种中断场景（初始分配、故障恢复、数据质量重分配）均简化为对剩余目标和活跃平台的重新拍卖，无需修改Aerostack2核心代码，也无需地面站参与。

Conclusion: SwarmLink可有效消除集中式单点故障，实现无人机群在光伏电站巡检等大规模遥感任务中的自主弹性协调。

Abstract: Deploying multiple UAVs for remote sensing enables proportional reductions in mission time, but realizing these benefits requires the fleet to coordinate at runtime: distributing sensing targets, responding to platform failures, and recovering from degraded data quality. In inspection campaigns, where mission value depends on complete coverage and the usability of every capture, a centralized ground-station coordinator is a single point of failure: a lost link or station fault leaves sensing gaps that cannot be filled without operator intervention. We propose the \textbf{SwarmLink}, an inter-agent communication infrastructure that non-invasively extends any existing aerial framework with peer-to-peer coordination capability, without modifying the host system. We apply it to photovoltaic plant inspection as a representative large-scale sensing campaign, extending Aerostack2 with a distributed auction that unifies initial sensing-target allocation, platform-failure recovery, and data-quality-triggered reassignment into a single runtime mechanism. All three disruption scenarios reduce to the same re-auction over remaining targets and active platforms, requiring zero modifications to the Aerostack2 core and no ground-station involvement during the mission.

</details>


### [130] [τ: Learning Touch-Augmented Vision-Language-Action Models from Future Visual Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24485)
*Ning Cheng,Jinan Xu,Wanlin Li,Yangzhi Chen,Jing Gao,Yiqun Wang,Kelan Peng,Wenjuan Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出τ框架，通过JEPA-inspired的未来视觉监督学习触觉表征，并与视觉-语言特征融合，在有限数据下提升机器人操作性能；同时发布TacAura多模态触觉数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在将触觉表征适配到预训练VLA模型时面临数据和建模双重挑战：数据层面，任务演示有限制约表征质量，大规模预训练成本高昂；建模层面，现有方法要么关注瞬时接触状态，要么用6D力/力矩序列建模时序交互，但对高维触觉信号的利用不足。

Method: 提出τ框架：基于Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA)思想，从未来视觉监督中学习动作条件化的时空触觉表征，并在潜在空间进行监督（仅训练阶段使用，无部署开销）；随后将触觉表征与视觉-语言特征融合用于动作生成。同时构建TacAura数据集，包含视觉、本体感知和触觉信号的同步数据。

Result: 实验表明τ在多个接触密集型操作任务中优于现有模型，并能泛化到未见过的物体和场景，表现出更强的操作性能和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 提出的触觉增强VLA框架能有效学习高维触觉表征并与视觉-语言特征融合，在有限数据条件下显著提升机器人操作能力，且具有良好的泛化性。

Abstract: Learning the informative tactile representation while effectively adapting it to pretrained Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models remains challenging at both the data and modeling levels. At the data level, limited task-specific demonstrations constrain representation quality, whereas large-scale pretraining incurs substantial costs. At the modeling level, existing methods either focus on instantaneous contact states or model temporal interaction dynamics using 6D wrench sequences, leaving high-dimensional tactile signals underexplored. To address these challenges, we present τ, a touch-augmented VLA framework that learns an action-conditioned spatiotemporal tactile representation from future visual supervision inspired by the Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA), and fuses it with vision-language features for action generation under limited data. This supervision operates in latent space and is used only during training, adding no deployment overhead. We also introduce TacAura, a dataset of synchronized vision, proprioception, and vision-based tactile signals across four representative contact-rich manipulation tasks. Experiments show that τ outperforms existing models and generalizes to unseen objects and scenes, delivering improved manipulation performance and robustness

</details>


### [131] [KAI: A Kinematic-Aware Interface for Data-Efficient Articulated Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24493)
*Yaping Li,Zhaxizhuoma,Qiaojun Yu,Jia Zeng,Dahua Lin,Jiangmiao Pang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为KAI的中间表征，通过嵌入可解释的运动学先验知识，有效提升了铰接物体操作策略的样本效率，在仅使用一半演示数据的情况下达到或超越基线性能，并展现出良好的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从机器人演示中学习铰接物体的运动学结构既困难又昂贵，现有方法样本效率低，需要大量数据才能有效学习。

Method: 设计Kinematic-Aware Articulation Interface (KAI)，一种结构化中间表征，嵌入可解释的几何和运动学先验知识，为策略学习提供强归纳偏置，并与人类交互视频进行协同训练。

Result: 在六项仿真任务中仅用一半演示数据达到82.9%平均成功率，匹配或超越基线；在杂乱真实场景中展现出良好的泛化能力；结合视频协同训练后，在多种视觉干扰下平均成功率超过70%。

Conclusion: KAI通过运动学感知的中间表征设计，显著提升了样本效率和泛化能力，且其动作无关的特性支持与人类视频协同训练，是一种有效的铰接物体操作学习方案。

Abstract: Articulated object manipulation requires an understanding of kinematic structure that is difficult and costly to learn from robot demonstrations alone. We introduce the Kinematic-Aware Articulation Interface (KAI), a structured intermediate representation that captures the kinematic structure of articulated objects. By embedding interpretable geometric and kinematic priors into policy learning, KAI provides a strong inductive bias aligned with the underlying structure of articulated motion. This design effectively improves sample efficiency, with gains particularly pronounced in low-data regimes: across six simulation tasks, our method achieves an average success rate of 82.9%, matching or surpassing baseline performance while using only half the demonstration data. Our method also exhibits robust generalization to unseen backgrounds and visual distractors, transferring from a single clean training environment to cluttered real-world scenes. KAI's action-agnostic design further enables co-training with human interaction videos to enhance real-world robustness: under diverse visual distractions, our method with video co-training achieves over 70% average success rate.

</details>


### [132] [NEO: NeRF It Once, Edit It Many Times for Continuous Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24538)
*Mikołaj Zieliński,David Hall,Dominik Belter,Peyman Moghadam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出NEO框架，将语言引导的NeRF编辑应用于机器人操作，在物体移除、场景编辑和抓取放置任务中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NeRF编辑方法在机器人操作场景中存在视觉不一致、几何伪影等问题，缺乏专门针对机器人操作评估的基准。

Method: (1) 语言引导的物体移除：神经场重采样+多视图一致渐进式修复；(2) 直接NeRF权重编辑：知识蒸馏+教师-学生模型，组合原始与编辑后的NeRF；(3) 构建NEO-Dataset评估基准。

Result: 在场景编辑任务（物体移除、抓取放置）中优于SOTA基线，生成视觉连贯且几何一致的编辑结果，减少伪影。

Conclusion: NEO框架有效实现了语言引导的NeRF场景编辑，为机器人操作提供了更可靠的场景建模能力。

Abstract: In this paper, we present NEO, a unified framework providing language-guided NeRF editing for robotic manipulation. Our paper introduces (i) a language-guided object removal that combines neural field resampling with multiview-consistent progressive inpainting, (ii) a direct NeRF weight editing method utilizing knowledge distillation, composing original and edited NeRFs via a teacher-student model, enabling coherent modeling of future scene states before a robot executes an action, and (iii) the first benchmark (NEO-Dataset) for quantitatively evaluating NeRF scene editing methods suitable for robot manipulation. We show that our approach outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in scene editing tasks, including object removal and pick-and-place robotic experiments, yielding visually coherent and geometrically consistent edits that reduce artifacts commonly introduced by prior methods.

</details>


### [133] [Development of a Handheld Actuation Mechanism for a Tendon-driven Robotically Steered Guidewire](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24629)
*Saima Chavenet,Timothy A. Brumfiel,Revanth Konda,Jaydev P. Desai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文开发了一种手持式驱动装置，用于肌腱驱动的机器人导丝，通过紧凑的卷绕机制实现电机驱动导丝转向及手动旋转/平移，并在一体化手柄上通过摇杆和开关控制进给、旋转和弯曲运动，在真实主动脉模型上验证了导航能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统血管内介入手术中，手动导航导丝因血管迂曲和导丝尖端可控性不足而具有挑战性，易导致血管损伤、穿孔、夹层、闭塞及术后血栓等并发症，亟需提升导丝操控性的解决方案。

Method: 设计了一种紧凑型卷绕机制集成于手持设备中，允许临床医生在握持设备时通过手柄上的摇杆和瞬时开关执行导丝的进给、旋转和弯曲运动，支持长达1.5米的机器人可转向导丝控制。

Result: 在解剖学上精确的主动脉体模模型中成功验证了所提设备的导航能力。

Conclusion: 该手持式驱动装置结合了电机驱动导丝转向与手动操作灵活性，有望降低血管介入手术的难度和并发症风险。

Abstract: An endovascular intervention begins with a skilled clinician manually navigating a long, slender wire, called a guidewire, to the target location within the vasculature. Due to factors, such as vessel tortuosity and lack of steerability at the guidewire tip, manual navigation of a guidewire could be challenging, potentially resulting in vessel damage, perforation, dissection, and occlusion, as well as postsurgical complications, such as thrombosis. This work details the development of a handheld actuation mechanism for tendon-driven robotic guidewires by utilizing the aforementioned spooling mechanism. Incorporating the compact spooling mechanism into a handheld device enables clinicians to leverage both motorized guidewire steering and manual rotation and translation, if desired. The proposed device is designed such that, while holding the device, the clinician can execute feeding, rotation, and bending motions for up to 1.5 m of the robotically steerable guidewire using a joystick and momentary switch on the handle. Furthermore, the proposed device is demonstrated through navigation in an anatomically accurate phantom aorta model.

</details>


### [134] [Data Pyramid for Embodied Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24744)
*Yifan Ye,Yankai Fu,Yaoxu Lv,Bohan Hou,Jun Cen,Lingdong Kong,Duo Zheng,Tianxing Chen,Jiaming Liu,Ziang Cao,Yunfan Lou,Wei Chow,Xian Sun,Yingshuo Wang,Kuangzhi Ge,Xiaowei Chi,Xidong Zhang,Zhibo Pang,Yiwu Zhong,Sirui Han,Zhihe Lu,Weihao Yuan,Qifeng Chen,Michael Yu Wang,Yao Mu,Ziwei Liu,Jianfei Yang,Ping Luo,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统梳理了具身智能体数据生态的五层金字塔结构（真实机器人数据、UMI数据、自我中心/外部中心数据、仿真数据、通用视觉语言数据），分析了各类数据在可扩展性与机器人对齐之间的权衡，并探讨了现有具身基础模型的数据配方及未来六大挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能体需要耦合观测、物理状态和动作的数据，但这类数据难以像多模态基础模型那样通过互联网规模的数据捷径获取。论文旨在系统化梳理具身数据来源，为下一代具身系统设计提供数据层面的指导。

Method: 提出具身数据生态的"金字塔"框架，按可扩展性与机器人对齐的张力量纲，将数据分为五层（真实机器人数据、UMI式数据、自我中心/外部中心数据、仿真数据、通用视觉语言数据），并从数据质量、多样性、可复用性和物理保真度四个维度进行刻画。随后通过数据配方的视角分析具身基础模型。

Result: 识别出现有具身基础模型（视觉-语言-动作模型、世界-动作模型等）在数据选择、对齐和混合方面的不同策略，并归纳了感知、推理、规划、动作生成和世界预测等能力与数据组成之间的关联。

Conclusion: 指出六大开放挑战：大规模触觉数据集构建、失败与恢复数据收集、可扩展数据采集管线、跨本体的动作对齐、利用自我中心数据实现灵巧操作、以及设计原则性的机器人学习数据配方。

Abstract: Multimodal foundation models learned to see and to speak by consuming the whole internet. Embodied agents admit no such shortcut, since they require data that couple observations with physical states and actions. These signals can be provided, to varying degrees, by multiple data sources. In this work, we organize the embodied data ecosystem as a "pyramid" spanning five complementary sources: real-robot data, UMI-style data, egocentric and exocentric data, simulation data, and general vision-language data. We organize the pyramid around the tension between scalability and robot alignment, and further characterize each source in terms of data quality, diversity, reusability, and physical fidelity. We then analyze recent embodied foundation models through the lens of their data recipes, examining how different sources are selected, aligned, and mixed during pretraining. For embodied brain models, vision-language-action models, and world-action models alike, we relate data composition to capabilities in perception, reasoning, planning, action generation, and world prediction. We close by discussing six open challenges: building large-scale tactile datasets, collecting failure and recovery data, developing scalable data-collection pipelines, aligning actions across embodiments, leveraging egocentric data for dexterous manipulation, and designing principled data recipes for robot learning. We hope this work paves the foundation for the design of next-generation embodied systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [135] [Concept-based Visual Counterfactual Explanations with Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22544)
*Yassine Oueslati,Daniil Kirilenko,Martin Gjoreski,Marc Langheinrich*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出C-VCE框架，通过概念瓶颈层将分类器内置于扩散模型中，无需外部噪声鲁棒分类器即可生成视觉反事实解释，在保持图像质量的同时实现可控的概念级编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的反事实解释方法依赖外部分类器，而分类器在噪声图像上不可靠，导致解释脆弱且难以部署。

Method: 在扩散模型中引入概念瓶颈层，将分类器内置；添加概率正则化项平衡预测改变与图像保持；使用梯度掩码限制修改区域。

Result: 在CelebA等基准上，C-VCE匹配或提升翻转率，生成的反事实在视觉上更接近原始输入且失真更少。

Conclusion: 暴露并控制内部概念层是让生成模型更易理解、更安全使用的有效途径。

Abstract: Visual counterfactual explanations aim to answer "what minimal change to this image would flip the model's prediction?", and are increasingly important as vision models are deployed in safety-critical domains (e.g., medicine). Existing diffusion-based methods can produce realistic edits, but they rely on external classifiers that must work reliably on noisy images, which makes them fragile and hard to deploy for robust explanations. We introduce C-VCE, a new diffusion framework that builds the classifier directly into the generative model via a concept bottleneck layer, so that counterfactuals are guided by human-interpretable features (concepts) instead of a separate noise robust classifier that works with pixel-level edits. Our model lets users to toggle on/off semantic concepts during sampling, then minimally adjusts relevant image regions, while preserving the rest of the image, respecting feature correlations. To keep edits small and controlled, we add a simple probabilistic regularizer that balances "change the prediction" against "stay close to the original", plus a gradient-based mask that confines modifications to the most relevant regions. On benchmarks such as CelebA, C-VCE matches or improves flip rates while producing counterfactuals that are visually closer to the input and less distorted than baselines that depend on separate noisy-image classifiers. These properties make C-VCE a practical tool for vision systems where users need concrete "what-if" images without having to trust an additional, noise-robust classifier. More broadly, our results suggest that exposing and controlling an internal concept layer is a promising way to make powerful generative models easier to understand and safer to use.

</details>


### [136] [SeT-Diff: Towards Semantic Foundation Models for HPC Telemetry and Time-Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22548)
*Giovanni B. Esposito,Francesco Antici,Daniele Cesarini,Andrea Bartolini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SeT-Diff，首个基于扩散模型的计算节点数字孪生基础模型，利用传感器语义描述实现零样本任务泛化，在HPC系统重建、预测、虚拟传感等任务中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HPC机器学习方法依赖固定传感器变量的静态子集，任务变更或传感器变化时模型失效，需要一种灵活、泛化的数字孪生方法。

Method: 基于扩散模型，将生成过程条件化为每个传感器的语义描述，解耦系统动态与数据集结构，实现传感器无关的时间序列建模。

Result: 重建任务MAE为0.0470；零样本传感器重排下精度几乎无损；单一模型可在数据插补、预测、虚拟传感（热推断MAE 0.033）上有效工作。

Conclusion: SeT-Diff是一种高效的数据驱动型HPC数字孪生方案，具备传感器排列稳定性与多任务泛化能力。

Abstract: Data centers and their compute nodes require accurate and flexible digital twins capable of modeling the complex interplay of workloads, environmental parameters, and physical metrics. Current machine learning approaches for HPC and its telemetry typically rely on a static subset of anonymous, fixed-position sensor variables tailored to single tasks. Consequently, these models become obsolete when target tasks change or sensor metrics vary. We propose SeT-Diff, the first foundational model for compute node telemetry and time-series. Unlike rigid architectures, our diffusion-based approach conditions the generative process on each sensor's semantic description, decoupling the system dynamics from the structure of the dataset. Experiments on a real-world supercomputer dataset demonstrate a Mean Absolute Error (MAE) of 0.0470 on reconstruction tasks. SeT-Diff exhibits zero-shot permutation stability, maintaining accuracy with negligible degradation even when sensors are shuffled. A single pre-trained model effectively performs data imputation, forecasting, and virtual sensing - achieving a 0.033 MAE in thermal inference - making SeT-Diff an effective data-driven digital twin for HPC systems.

</details>


### [137] [QFoldAgent: An Autonomous Quantum Optimization Multi-Agent System for Protein Structure Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22549)
*Winson Chen,Yuqi Zhang,Sixu Chen,Nuo Xu,Qiang Guan,Caiwen Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出QFoldAgent，一个用于5残基四面体格点折叠的闭环多智能体框架，通过设计智能体、VQE量子-经典管道和反馈智能体的协同迭代，自动优化哈密顿量惩罚权重，在量子噪声环境下显著提升蛋白质结构预测精度和结构有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合量子-经典蛋白质结构预测方法严重依赖人工设定的哈密顿量惩罚权重，且仅在极短片段上进行模拟评估，缺乏系统化的权重优化机制。

Method: 提出QFoldAgent多智能体框架：设计智能体提出序列条件惩罚权重，VQE量子-经典管道在Qiskit Aer噪声下优化哈密顿量，反馈智能体利用能量景观诊断和MolProbity验证信号迭代改进权重。

Result: 在QDockBank基准上中位RMSD从3.64Å降至3.20Å；在未见序列上结构有效性从87.5%提升至98.7%，恢复87%初始无效病例，最强的控制器在87%序列上改善cycle-3能量同时保持96%拉氏图偏好几何。

Conclusion: 迭代智能体控制能够系统性地改善优化行为并减少5残基量子设置中的失败案例。

Abstract: Hybrid quantum-classical protein structure prediction depends strongly on Hamiltonian penalty weights, yet existing lattice-based workflows typically fix these coefficients by hand and evaluate only very short fragments in simulation. We present QFoldAgent, a closed-loop multi-agent framework for 5-residue tetrahedral-lattice folding in which a design agent proposes sequence-conditioned penalties, a VQE-based quantum-classical pipeline optimizes the resulting Hamiltonian under Qiskit Aer noise, and a feedback agent uses energy-landscape diagnostics and MolProbity validation signals to refine penalties across cycles. Ground-truth metrics such as RMSD are never exposed to the agents and are used only for evaluation. We study the framework on two complementary datasets: 55 QDockBank-derived fragments with known structures and 100 coverage-optimized unseen sequences. On the QDockBank benchmark, QFoldAgent reduces median RMSD from 3.64 Å to 3.20 Å, with the largest gains on the hardest targets. On unseen sequences, the closed loop raises structural validity from 87.5% to 98.7%, recovers 87% of initially invalid cases, and the strongest controller improves cycle-3 energy on 87% of sequences while maintaining 96% Ramachandran-favored geometry. These results show that iterative agent control can systematically improve optimization behavior and reduce failure cases in a 5-residue quantum setting.

</details>


### [138] [Same Question, Different Answers: Evaluating LLM Reliability Beyond Accuracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22554)
*Kazem Faghih,Yize Cheng,Shoumik Saha,Mobina Pournemat,Armin Gerami,Soheil Feizi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLMs在不同但等价的问法下，答案常常不一致，单次提示的正确性不可靠，但通过自我释义策略可部分恢复潜在知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLMs在等价但不同措辞的问题下，答案是否稳定可靠，因为标准准确率指标可能掩盖了模型的不稳定性。

Method: 在4个基准测试和13个模型上，对事实问答和数学推理任务进行意义保持的释义变换，比较模型输出的稳定性，并提出了自我释义策略来提升性能。

Result: 整体准确率变化不大，但实例级行为不稳定，答案翻转率高达23%以上；单次正确性常不可靠，但模型对至少一种释义通常能给出正确答案。

Conclusion: 标准准确率指标掩盖了模型的不稳定性，评估等价输入下的一致性才能更全面反映LLM的可靠性；自我释义策略可以有效恢复潜在知识。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often achieve strong accuracy on benchmarks, yet it remains unclear how reliably they apply this knowledge when the same question is phrased in different but equivalent ways. In this work, we study how model answers change under meaning-preserving paraphrases across factual question answering and mathematical reasoning tasks. Across four benchmarks and 13 models, we find that model outputs frequently depend on the exact wording of the prompt. While overall accuracy typically changes only modestly across paraphrases, instance-level behavior is far less stable: for many questions, models alternate between correct and incorrect answers depending on phrasing, with mismatch rates reaching more than 23%. Conditioning on questions that are answered correctly in their original form reveals even larger failures measured by answer flip rates, showing that single-prompt correctness is often a poor indicator of reliability. At the same time, we find that models often produce a correct answer for at least one paraphrase of a question, suggesting that the underlying knowledge is present but inconsistently retrieved. Building on this observation, we show that a simple self-paraphrasing strategy can partially recover this latent knowledge and improve performance at inference time. Together, these findings suggest that standard accuracy metrics can mask substantial instability, and that evaluating consistency across equivalent inputs provides a clearer picture of LLM reliability.

</details>


### [139] [DeepLens Diagnosis Agent: Agentic Workflow Design Lets a Small Reasoning Model Compete with Frontier LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22555)
*Mahmood Bayeshi,Veysel Kocaman,Muhammed Ali Naqvi,Yigit Gul,David Talby*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DeepLens Diagnosis Agent，一个五阶段诊断流程，基于7B小模型+RAG，在DiagnosisArena基准上达到60.14%的top-1准确率，比无流程的同一模型高出36个百分点，且成本低于Claude Sonnet 4.5和Gemini 3.1 Pro。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿LLM虽然泛化能力强，但单次提示在医学诊断推理中表现脆弱，而诊断本身是多阶段复杂过程，现有方法缺乏结构化的流程约束和可审计性。

Method: 五阶段诊断流程：结构化临床提取、受控检索、约束候选生成、显式证据三角验证、可审计最终决策。核心使用JSL Medical Small 7B v2小模型+RAG。

Result: 在915例DiagnosisArena上top-1准确率60.14%，优于同规模模型；相比无流程版本提升36个百分点；每例成本$0.0072，延迟24秒，比Claude Sonnet 4.5和Gemini 3.1 Pro分别便宜35-45%且准确率高出9.7pp和9.17pp。

Conclusion: 不确定性下的诊断推理不仅需要知识回忆，更需要流程约束；流程设计可以超越参数规模和API成本；结构化中间产物支持高 stakes 场景下的可审计性和错误定位。

Abstract: Medical diagnosis is a multi-stage process: extract facts, consult knowledge, generate a differential analysis, and select the best diagnosis with explanations. Frontier LLMs are strong generalists, but single-shot prompting often yields brittle diagnostic reasoning. We present the DeepLens Diagnosis Agent, a five-stage harnessing pipeline (combining model capabilities with disciplined process constraints) centered on a small medical reasoning model (JSL Medical Small 7B v2) and retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). The pipeline enforces structured clinical extraction, disciplined retrieval, constrained candidate generation, explicit evidence triangulation, and an auditable final decision. On the 915-case DiagnosisArena benchmark, the agent achieved 60.14% top-1 diagnostic accuracy, the highest among small and medium-sized models. The same model without the agent workflow achieved 23.99%, a +36-point gain from workflow design alone, despite 88.2% on standard medical benchmarks, showing that diagnostic reasoning under uncertainty requires more than knowledge recall. The agent costs USD 0.0072 per case (24K tokens on A100) with 24-second latency, 35-45% cheaper than Claude Sonnet 4.5 (USD 0.0110) and Gemini 3.1 Pro (USD 0.0128) while outperforming them by +9.70pp and +9.17pp. Harnessing can also correct frontier model failures; workflow constraints can outweigh parameter count or API cost.
  Beyond aggregate accuracy, the pipeline produces structured intermediate artifacts that make each stage inspectable and support error localization. These properties support high-stakes settings where traceability, reproducibility, and auditable evidence matter alongside benchmark performance.

</details>


### [140] [MIITA: Memory-Induced Inference-Time Adaptation for Continual Learning with Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22556)
*Dong Li,Yanchi Liu,Xujiang Zhao,Wei Cheng,Zhengzhang Chen,Xintao Wu,Zhong Chen,Chen Zhao,Haifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MIITA框架，通过存储监督经验为紧凑的修正方向原型和语义锚点，在推理时基于语义和不确定性线索检索，并利用门控临时隐藏状态适配实现非破坏性的知识复用，解决小语言模型在资源受限条件下持续学习中的灾难性遗忘问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于记忆的持续学习方法针对大语言模型设计，依赖充足存储和强大的上下文推理能力，而小语言模型在资源受限部署中参数空间有限，直接更新会导致灾难性遗忘。

Method: MIITA框架：1) 将监督经验存储为紧凑的修正方向原型与语义锚点；2) 推理时基于语义和不确定性线索检索；3) 通过门控临时隐藏状态适配应用检索到的方向，无需更新骨干网络、扩展提示或测试时反向传播。

Result: 在多种监督持续学习设置下的广泛实验表明，MIITA在固定记忆预算下持续提升最终性能并缓解遗忘问题。

Conclusion: MIITA通过推理时自适应机制有效实现小语言模型的持续学习，为资源受限场景提供了一种轻量级、非破坏性的知识保留方案。

Abstract: Continual learning (CL) is essential for small language models (SLMs) to adapt to evolving real-world needs in resource-constrained deployments. However, directly updating their limited parameter space causes catastrophic forgetting. While memory-based methods naturally address this by decoupling knowledge retention from parameters, existing approaches designed for large language models (LLMs) rely on abundant storage and strong in-context reasoning that SLMs lack. To address these challenges, we propose MIITA, a Memory-Induced Inference-Time Adaptation framework for supervised CL under constrained storage. MIITA stores supervised experiences as compact correction-direction prototypes with semantic anchors, and retrieves them at inference time using semantic and uncertainty-based cues. The retrieved directions are applied through gated temporary hidden-state adaptation, enabling non-destructive reuse of past supervision without backbone updates, prompt extensions, or test-time backpropagation. A local theoretical analysis links this design to first-order loss reduction, uncertainty-guided retrieval, and directional coverage for retaining old-stage knowledge. Extensive experiments across diverse supervised CL settings show that MIITA consistently improves final performance and mitigates forgetting under fixed memory budgets.

</details>


### [141] [Codifying the Judge: Scalable Evaluation via Program Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22561)
*Tzu-Heng Huang,Shengqi Qiu,Frederic Sala*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出程序蒸馏方法，将LLM评估逻辑蒸馏为程序委员会，实现透明、高效、低成本的自动评估系统PAJAMA，在多个数据集上匹配13B参数LLM的评估性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-a-judge评估模式成本高、延迟大、决策不透明，限制了其可扩展性和可靠性，亟需更高效透明的替代方案。

Method: 通过程序蒸馏将LLM的决策逻辑转化为可编程评分委员会，PAJAMA系统合成评判程序并聚合决策，同时引入回退机制处理低置信度案例。

Result: 在5个数据集和4个模型族上，程序化评判器匹配13B参数LLM法官的性能；作为奖励信号时，在RewardBench上以两个数量级更低的API成本超越专有LLM训练的信号。

Conclusion: 程序蒸馏提供了一种透明、高效、低成本的自动评估替代方案，改进了准确率和吞吐量的帕累托前沿。

Abstract: LLM-as-a-judge has become the standard for automated evaluation, but it suffers from high cost, significant latency, and opaque decisions -- limitations that undermine its scalability and reliability. We address these with a simple, efficient alternative: program distillation. Instead of prompting an LLM at the evaluation time, we distill its decision logic into a committee of programs that score candidates directly. These programmatic judges offer transparency, are easily inspected or edited, and eliminate per-sample API costs. Building on this notion, we introduce PAJAMA, a system that synthesizes programs as judges, aggregates their decisions into a joint verdict, and incorporates a fallback mechanism to selectively escalate low-confidence cases to an LLM. Across five datasets and four model families, we show that programmatic judges can match the performance of a 13B-size LLM judge. When using program outputs as routing signals, PAJAMA improves both accuracy and throughput and advances the Pareto frontier. Beyond evaluation, programmatic judges produce cheap and effective reward signals: on RewardBench, a reward model distilled from programs' verdicts outperforms one trained on a proprietary LLM's labels at two orders of magnitude lower API cost.

</details>


### [142] [SF-AMS: Strategic Forgetting for Structured Memory in LLM Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22562)
*Ning Yang,Siqi Li,Miaoxin Shen,Yuan Zhou,Meng Zhang,Tong Li,Haijun Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SF-AMS提出了一种基于动态效用信号的选择性遗忘框架，通过建模记忆单元的长时重要性来维护紧凑高可用的智能体记忆，在多个长上下文推理任务上显著超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在处理长上下文时，冗余和无关信息会严重退化多步推理能力，现有静态检索和启发式衰减方法难以有效维护高效用记忆。

Method: SF-AMS框架通过效用驱动的生存机制，利用使用冗余和时序信号更新记忆重要性，形成分层记忆结构；同时结合复合重要性评分（语义+实体级信号）增强检索鲁棒性，实现选择性遗忘。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-7B上多跳推理提升+9.65 F1，GPT-4o-mini上时序推理+6.91 F1，开放域任务+6.53 F1，在LoCoMo和LongMemEval-s数据集上全面超越LightMem、MemO、A-Mem等基线。

Conclusion: 将记忆重要性建模为动态效用信号是可靠长上下文推理的关键，SF-AMS通过选择性遗忘机制有效解决了记忆冗余问题，并展现出强跨骨干网络泛化能力。

Abstract: Managing long-context dependencies remains a primary bottleneck in LLM agents, as redundant and irrelevant information can degrade multi-step reasoning. Strategic Forgetting for Agent Memory Systems (SF-AMS) is proposed as a framework for maintaining compact high-utility memory by modeling the long-term importance of memory units. SF-AMS replaces static retrieval and heuristic decay with a utility-driven survival mechanism that updates memory importance from usage redundancy and temporal signals, inducing a hierarchical memory structure that prioritizes stable entity-consistent information while filtering noise. On top of this, Composite Importance Scoring integrates semantic and entity level signals to improve retrieval robustness. Experiments on LoCoMo and LongMemEval-s show consistent gains over strong state of the art baselines including LightMem MemO and A-Mem. The largest improvement appears in multi-hop reasoning under Qwen2.5-7B where SF-AMS achieves plus 9.65 F1 over the strongest baseline followed by temporal reasoning under GPT-4o-mini plus 6.91 F1 and open-domain tasks plus 6.53 F1 demonstrating strong cross backbone generalization. These results show that modeling memory importance as a dynamic utility signal is critical for reliable long-context reasoning.

</details>


### [143] [Synthetic Scenario Generation for Evaluation of Industry 4.0 Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22563)
*Sagar Chethan Kumar,Rohith Kanathur,Dhaval Patel,Kaoutar El Maghraoui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ScenarioGeneratorAgent，通过合成数据生成方法扩展工业智能体基准测试AssetOpsBench，新增智能电网变压器资产类别及四项IEC诊断工具，并利用混合验证-修复循环和多项优化技术，将50个场景的生成时间缩短8倍，同时保持场景质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工业智能体基准测试（如AssetOpsBench）依赖人工编写场景，覆盖资产类别有限，缺乏可扩展性，难以满足现实评估需求。

Method: 提出ScenarioGeneratorAgent流水线，包括：基于证据的资产画像构建、覆盖感知的场景预算分配、混合验证-修复循环（保证模式有效性、工具可达性、物理合理性、标准对齐、去重），以及两级缓存、并行焦点组生成、线程池卸载、批量化LLM调用、早期拒绝过滤等优化策略。

Result: 在智能电网变压器场景生成中，优化后50个场景的端到端运行时间缩短8倍，复合质量评分74.2±1.9，与基线73.8±3.0相当。

Conclusion: 基于标准约束的合成场景生成方法能够高效扩展工业智能体基准测试，在不牺牲场景质量的前提下显著提升生成效率。

Abstract: Industrial agent benchmarks require realistic evaluation scenarios that integrate telemetry, failure modes, maintenance records, and domain standards. However, existing benchmarks such as AssetOpsBench rely on manually authored scenarios and cover a limited set of asset classes. We extend AssetOpsBench with a Smart Grid Transformer asset class and four IEC-grounded diagnostic tools for health-index prediction, dissolved-gas analysis, winding-temperature assessment, and load-profile assessment. We further introduce ScenarioGeneratorAgent, a pipeline for synthetic industrial-agent scenario generation. The pipeline constructs evidence-grounded asset profiles, allocates coverage-aware scenario budgets across operational domains, and generates candidates through a hybrid validation-and-repair loop that enforces schema validity, tool reachability, physical plausibility, standards alignment, and deduplication. To improve scalability, we apply two-level caching, parallel focus-group generation, thread-pool offloading, batched LLM calls, and early rejection filtering. On Smart Grid Transformer scenario generation, these optimizations reduce end-to-end runtime by $8\times$ for 50 scenarios while preserving quality, achieving a composite quality score of $74.2 \pm 1.9$ compared with $73.8 \pm 3.0$ for the unoptimized baseline. These results show that standards-grounded synthetic scenario generation can efficiently expand industrial-agent benchmarks without sacrificing scenario quality.

</details>


### [144] [Loss-Aware Feature-Map Pruning in Convolutional Neural Networks Using Multi-Armed Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22564)
*Salem Ameen,Sunil Vadera*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多臂老虎机的损失感知特征图剪枝框架，通过将每个候选特征图视为一个臂，利用UCB1和Thompson Sampling等算法评估移除特征图带来的损失变化，从而在保持模型精度的同时有效减少卷积计算量和存储成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卷积神经网络中常存在冗余特征图，增加了存储和推理成本，因此需要一种高效的剪枝方法在保持精度的同时降低计算开销。

Method: 将每个候选特征图视为一个臂，每次选择一张图临时掩码并在小批量数据上评估，观察损失变化并转化为安全移除奖励。经过固定预算后，根据学习到的分数永久移除top-k特征图及其对应的滤波器、偏置和下一层输入通道核。使用UCB1和Thompson Sampling进行决策。

Result: UCB1和Thompson Sampling在多个数据集（MNIST、CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100、SVHN、CUB-200-2011、Oxford Flowers 102）上均能在移除特征图并减少卷积计算的同时，保持接近未剪枝模型的精度。Friedman和Nemenyi检验显示UCB1获得最高平均秩，两者显著优于贪心和基于幅度的剪枝方法。

Conclusion: 基于多臂老虎机的损失感知特征图剪枝框架是一种有效的结构化剪枝方法，能够在保持模型精度的前提下有效减少冗余特征图，其中UCB1表现最优，Thompson Sampling次之。

Abstract: Convolutional neural networks often contain redundant feature maps that increase storage and inference cost. This paper presents a loss-aware feature-map pruning framework using multi-armed bandits. Feature-map pruning is structured because it removes complete convolutional output channels and their producing filters rather than isolated scalar weights. Each candidate feature map is treated as an arm. At each play time, one map is temporarily masked and evaluated on a sampled mini-batch; the map is then restored and the observed loss change is converted into a safe-removal reward. After a fixed play budget, candidate maps are ranked by learned scores and the top-k maps are permanently removed with their filters, biases and corresponding next-layer input-channel kernels. The study evaluates UCB1 and Thompson Sampling, compares them with direct/oracle-style evaluation on LeNet/MNIST, and extends the evaluation to MNIST, CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, SVHN, CUB-200-2011 and Oxford Flowers 102. Results show that UCB1 and Thompson Sampling preserve accuracy close to unpruned models while removing feature maps and reducing convolutional computation. Friedman and Nemenyi tests show that UCB1 obtains the highest mean rank, followed by Thompson Sampling; both significantly outperform greedy and magnitude-based pruning while remaining statistically comparable to the original unpruned model.

</details>


### [145] [DSTFView: Multi-View Cloud-Edge Workload Forecasting with Dual-Input Spatio-Temporal-Frequency Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22565)
*Qingzhong Li,Hui Ma,Yajun Zhang,Qingchang Ma,Zhou Long*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DSTFView框架，通过双输入时空频多视图建模，在云边协同环境下实现更准确的工作负载预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以在云边协同环境中平衡多维度特征建模与预测效率，无法很好地支持边缘AI推理对低延迟、高并发和高可靠性的需求。

Method: 提出DSTFView框架，采用双输入结构联合建模邻近性和周期性依赖，提取空间、时间和频域特征，并设计自适应融合机制动态调整各视图贡献以捕捉突变。

Result: 在CPU和TP数据集上，DSTFView在多个预测时间跨度和评估指标上均优于代表性基线方法。

Conclusion: DSTFView能有效解决云边协同环境下工作负载预测的多维度建模难题，显著提升预测性能。

Abstract: With the widespread deployment of edge-side AI inference, edge platforms are increasingly required to support latency-sensitive, highly concurrent, and reliability-critical applications. However, existing methods often struggle to balance multidimensional feature modeling and forecasting efficiency in collaborative cloud-edge environments. To address this issue, we propose DSTFView, a dual-input spatio-temporal-frequency multi-view workload forecasting framework for collaborative cloud-edge environments. It jointly models closeness and period dependencies and extracts spatial, temporal, and frequency-domain dependencies. Besides, it designs an adaptive fusion mechanism and adjusts the contribution of each view to capture abrupt changes. Experimental results on the CPU and TP datasets demonstrate that DSTFView consistently outperforms representative baselines across multiple forecasting horizons and evaluation metrics.

</details>


### [146] [MedLoCoMo: A Long-Context Multi-Session Medical Dialogue Benchmark for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22566)
*Zeyu Zhang,Ziqing Wang,Kaize Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MedLoCoMo是一个面向多次入院患者临床推理的医学长上下文记忆基准，包含100条患者时间线，平均每条对话约74,512个token，实验发现跨入院推理比单次入院证据使用更具挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学QA基准主要测试短上下文知识或单文档推理，缺乏评估LLM在纵向患者历史中使用、连接及合理拒答能力的基准。

Method: 基于MIMIC-IV构建入院级临床数据包，合成医患对话，生成单次入院、跨入院及对抗性不可回答三种类型的证据关联QA样本。

Result: 100条患者时间线，平均每条含1,669.8轮对话、29.7次会话、74,512.2个token；跨入院推理一致困难于局部证据使用。

Conclusion: 即使模型拥有长上下文窗口或使用外部记忆/检索方法，跨入院推理仍然是更大挑战，该基准已开源以促进可重复性研究。

Abstract: MedLoCoMo is a Medical Long-Context Memory benchmark for patient-specific clinical reasoning over multi-admission medical dialogue. Existing medical QA benchmarks largely test short context knowledge or single document grounding, leaving open whether LLMs can use, connect, and abstain over longitudinal patient histories. We build MedLoCoMo from deidentified MIMIC-IV and MIMIC-IV-Note records by constructing admission-level clinical packets, synthesizing grounded doctor-patient conversations, and generating evidence linked QA items over single-admission, cross-admission, and adversarial unanswerable settings. The benchmark contains 100 patient timelines averaging 1,669.8 turns, 29.7 sessions, and 74,512.2 tokens per conversation. Across the evaluated baselines, cross-admission reasoning is consistently harder than localized evidence use, even when models have long context windows or use external memory or retrieval methods. The code and MedLoCoMo benchmark release is available at https://github.com/leozzy13/MedLoCoMo for use and reproducibility.

</details>


### [147] [Keyword Matters: Unveiling the Energy Sensitivity of On-Device LLM Prompting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22568)
*Ruiyi Tao,Xiaolong Tu,Haoxin Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过实证研究揭示了提示词设计（如动词选择和指令结构）对移动端大语言模型能耗的显著影响，发现提示工程可作为提升能效的轻量级手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注模型压缩和运行时加速，但提示词设计对设备端大语言模型能耗的影响尚未被充分探索。

Method: 在智能手机上通过真实功率测量，量化分析提示词的语言特征（尤其是祈使关键词和指令结构）如何影响解码长度和总能耗。

Result: 不同动词和任务之间存在一致的能耗差异，提示词措辞显著影响解码长度和总能耗。

Conclusion: 提示工程是一种轻量级的能效优化手段，可通过优化提示词设计显著降低移动端大语言模型的能耗。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed on mobile and embedded devices to improve privacy and reduce network latency.
