Result: 在22个留出基准测试中，Semalith v1.4在7/7的提示注入评估和11/18的整体基准上优于Llama-Guard-3-8B，参数仅为后者1/44。对208个良性智能体提示的FPR为0.000，而Llama-Guard为0.063。在一般危害基准上Llama-Guard领先。

Conclusion: v1.3推荐用于对话审核场景（ToxicChat F1 0.624），v1.4推荐用于需要BFSI标签覆盖或对良性智能体提示零误报的场景。

Abstract: Deploying large language models in financial-services and agentic settings requires safety classifiers that simultaneously handle prompt injection, regulatory compliance, and general harm, a combination no existing open guardrail addresses in a single inference pass.
  Semalith v1.4 is a 184M-parameter DeBERTa-v3-base classifier performing simultaneous three-axis safety classification including prompt injection, general harm, and financial-services regulatory compliance, in a single forward pass. Its 22-class head (BENIGN, nine prompt-injection sub-types, general-harm, eleven BFSI labels) is trained with a 4-class auxiliary super-category head under jointly weighted loss, on a 76,204-row corpus mined from 49 public sources with SHA-1 deduplication against every held-out evaluation set, with 21 of 22 benchmarks at zero contamination (max 0.22%).
  Against Llama-Guard-3-8B on 22 held-out benchmarks, Semalith v1.4 wins every prompt-injection evaluation (7/7) and 11 of 18 benchmarks overall at 44x fewer parameters, with FPR = 0.000 on 208 benign agentic prompts vs 0.063 for Llama-Guard-3-8B. On general-harm benchmarks (WildGuardMix, HEx-PHI, HarmBench), Llama-Guard-3 leads; this complementary split is documented in Section 4. Six measured weak spots are disclosed in Section 6.
  Deployment guidance: v1.3 is recommended for conversational moderation deployments (ToxicChat F1 0.624); v1.4 is recommended when BFSI label coverage or zero-FPR on benign agentic prompts is the priority.

</details>


### [222] [CORVUS: Context Optimization and Reduction Via Underlying Synchronization for LLM Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22711)
*Mingwei Zheng,David OBrien,Siwei Cui,Pardis Pashakhanloo,Rajdeep Mukherjee,Myeongsoo Kim,Sachit Kuhar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CORVUS提出了一种新的轨迹架构，通过将文件读取操作与其观察结果解耦，并维护文件同步注册表，从而减轻了LLM编码智能体在推理过程中因文件快照过时和冗余读取导致的轨迹膨胀问题，在多个基准测试中实现了9-50%的输入token减少和高达37%的推理周期缩减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的追加式轨迹架构将文件读取与观察结果紧密耦合，导致文件快照随着编辑或并发修改而过时，引发推理错误和冗余的文件重读，每次重读又进一步增加轨迹负担。

Method: CORVUS提出解耦文件读取与观察结果的新轨迹架构，通过维护相关文件的同步注册表，在每个推理周期只注入文件当前内容，确保轨迹轻量化并与实际代码库状态保持同步。

Result: 在SWE-POLYBENCH_VERIFIED和SWE-BENCH PRO上，跨4个LLM实现了平均输入token减少9-50%，最终提示缩短15-32%，推理周期减少最高达37%，同时保持了相当的通过率。

Conclusion: 解耦文件读取与观察结果的轨迹架构能有效消除冗余文件副本和过时快照，在不牺牲性能的前提下显著减轻轨迹负担，提升编码智能体的效率。

Abstract: LLM coding agents operate by constructing trajectories that accumulate reasoning, tool calls, and results to enable multi-step decision-making. However, the conventional append-only trajectory architecture found in practice tightly couples file-read actions with their observations, capturing snapshots that become permanently fixed in the chronological history. As files change through agent edits or concurrent human modifications, these snapshots become stale, causing reasoning errors and causing agents to redundantly re-read files, with each re-read appending yet another copy to the trajectory. To mitigate this, we propose CORVUS, a novel trajectory architecture that decouples file-read actions from their observations by maintaining a synchronized registry of relevant files and injecting only their current contents at each reasoning cycle. This structural change produces significantly lighter-weight trajectories that remain synchronized with the actual codebase state by construction, eliminating redundant file copies and stale snapshots that bloat conventional trajectories. We evaluated CORVUS on SWE- POLYBENCH_VERIFIED and SWE-BENCH PRO across four LLMs, achieving 9-50% reduction in average input tokens per task, 15-32% shorter final prompts, and up to 37% fewer reasoning cycles while maintaining comparable pass rates.

</details>


### [223] [CausalGate: Causal Importance Distillation for Transformer Module Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22720)
*Kiran Nair,Smriti Regmi,Rodrigue Rizk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CausalGate框架，通过干预引导的方式测量Transformer各子层对语义的因果贡献，并蒸馏为静态标量门控，实现零开销的高效推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自适应推理方法依赖隐藏状态相似度等观测启发式指标，难以捕捉对语义准确性至关重要的非线性结构计算。

Method: 在校准阶段，通过零化各Attention和MLP子层输出并用KL散度测量语义损伤，构建结构重要性层级；利用指数移动平均和可微成对排序损失蒸馏为静态轻量标量门控。

Result: 在TinyLlama-1.1B、Qwen2.5-3B和Llama-3.1-8B上，CausalGate在语言建模和常识推理基准中一致优于动态路由和层跳过基线，实现了硬件延迟降低。

Conclusion: CausalGate通过因果干预而非相关性度量来识别冗余模块，实现了零运行开销的高效Transformer推理。

Abstract: Existing adaptive inference methods for Large Language Models rely on observational heuristics, such as hidden-state similarity or activation magnitudes, to drop redundant modules. However, these correlation-based metrics often fail to capture subtle, non-linear structural computations vital for semantic accuracy. We introduce CausalGate, an intervention-guided framework for compute-efficient transformer inference. During a calibration phase, CausalGate isolates individual Attention and MLP sub-layers, zeros out their respective outputs, and measures the exact semantic damage via the Kullback-Leibler divergence of the final logit distribution. To eliminate runtime routing overhead, this structural importance hierarchy is distilled into a global set of static, lightweight scalar gates using an Exponential Moving Average smoothing objective paired with a differentiable pairwise ranking loss. Evaluated on TinyLlama-1.1B, Qwen2.5-3B, and Llama-3.1-8B across language modeling and commonsense reasoning benchmarks, CausalGate consistently outperforms prominent dynamic routing and layer-skipping baselines, translating theoretical compute savings into concrete hardware latency reductions with zero operational overhead.

</details>


### [224] [Progress-conditioned Group Policy Optimization for Long-Horizon Agentic Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22724)
*Kaibing Yang,Guangfeng Cai,Shengtian Yang,Shuo He,Yu Li,Mengyi Liu,Pengwei Chen,Jun Xu,Lei Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对组策略优化在长周期任务中因采样不平衡导致的“信用陷阱”问题，提出ProGPO方法，通过在无奖励组中引入首次访问观测覆盖率作为相对优势信号，打破失败主导的循环，在ALFWorld和WebShop上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有组策略优化在长周期困难任务中面临采样不平衡问题：重复或低效动作占据高概率区域，而有用的状态改变动作采样不足，导致大量全失败组无法提供有效梯度校正，形成自增强的信用陷阱。

Method: 提出ProGPO（Progress-conditioned Group Policy Optimization），核心思想是当组内所有样本均获得零结果奖励时，利用首次访问观测覆盖率来分配相对优势，即访问更多新状态的轨迹或步骤获得更高优势值，因为到达新观测是任务成功的前提。

Result: 在ALFWorld和WebShop两个具有挑战性的Agent基准上，使用Qwen2.5-1.5B/7B-Instruct模型，ProGPO一致优于组策略优化基线，在困难任务上提升尤为显著。

Conclusion: ProGPO通过引入基于进度的内在奖励信号，有效解决了组策略优化在长周期任务中的信用分配问题，为在稀疏奖励环境下训练LLM智能体提供了新思路。

Abstract: Group-based policy optimization has been increasingly used to train large language model (LLM) agents from sparse outcome rewards by comparing trajectories or steps within a group. However, on difficult long-horizon tasks, this comparison can suffer from a sampling imbalance: repeated or low-effect actions dominate the high-probability region of the policy while useful state-changing actions remain under-sampled. This imbalance produces many all-failed rollout groups, where outcome rewards provide no direction for correcting the policy. Together, these effects can form a self-reinforcing credit trap: failure-dominated sampling yields no outcome-based correction, allowing repeated low-effect actions to persist. To break this loop, we propose Progress-conditioned Group Policy Optimization (ProGPO), which uses first-visit observation coverage only when all samples in a group receive zero outcome reward. Specifically, within such groups, ProGPO assigns higher relative advantages to trajectories or steps that visit more new states since reaching new observations is a prerequisite for task success. Experiments on two challenging agentic benchmarks, ALFWorld and WebShop with Qwen2.5-1.5/7B-Instruct, show that ProGPO consistently improves over group-based baselines, with particularly large gains on hard tasks.

</details>


### [225] [QFedPolyp: A Communication- and Inference-Efficient Federated Learning Framework for Polyp Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22743)
*Madan Baduwal,Priyanka Paudel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QFedPolyp提出了一种结合量化感知训练与低精度模型通信的联邦学习框架，在保持隐私的同时，将通信成本降低约4倍，推理速度提升最高1.5倍，适用于协作式息肉分割任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集中式深度学习需要共享敏感医疗数据，存在隐私风险；联邦学习虽能保护隐私，但全精度模型参数的反复传输带来高通信成本。QFedPolyp旨在解决隐私保护与通信效率之间的平衡问题。

Method: QFedPolyp将量化感知训练与低精度模型通信相结合。每家医院在本地私有数据上训练轻量级U-Net，训练过程中模拟量化操作；客户端传输量化后的模型参数至中心服务器，服务器通过联邦平均算法（Federated Averaging）进行重构与聚合。

Result: 全精度联邦训练在Kvasir-SEG和CVC-ClinicVideoDB上分别达到0.910和0.930的Dice分数；均匀8位通信将传输成本降低约4倍，同时保持竞争力分割精度；量化模型推理速度比全精度模型快最高1.5倍。

Conclusion: QFedPolyp实现了隐私保护的协作式息肉分割，同时降低了通信开销并提升了推理速度，生成的轻量级模型适用于实时临床部署。

Abstract: Background and Objective: Automatic polyp segmentation supports computer-aided diagnosis and early colorectal cancer detec- tion. Centralized deep learning requires hospitals to share sensitive medical data, while federated learning preserves privacy but introduces high communication costs through repeated transmission of full-precision model parameters. We propose QFedPolyp, a communication- and inference-efficient federated learning framework for collaborative polyp segmentation.
  Methods: QFedPolyp combines quantization-aware training with low-precision model communication. Each hospital locally trains a lightweight U-Net on private data while simulating quantization during training. Clients transmit quantized model parameters to a central server, where they are reconstructed and aggregated using Federated Averaging. Evaluation is performed on Kvasir-SEG, CVC-ClinicVideoDB, PolypGen, and BKAI-IGH NeoPolyp.
  Results: Full-precision federated training achieves Dice scores of 0.910 on Kvasir-SEG and 0.930 on CVC-ClinicVideoDB. Uni- form 8-bit communication reduces transmission cost by approximately 4 times while preserving competitive segmentation accuracy. Quantized models also achieve up to 1.5 times faster inference than full-precision models.
  Conclusions: QFedPolyp enables privacy-preserving collaborative polyp segmentation with reduced communication overhead and faster inference. The resulting lightweight models are suitable for real-time clinical deployment.

</details>


### [226] [Learning to Access Computation: Accessibility Plasticity as a Principle of Adaptive Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22748)
*Zhaowen Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"可访问性可塑性"（Accessibility Plasticity）这一新的自适应计算原则，强调系统不仅可以通过改变计算能力来适应，还可以通过重新组织现有计算之间的交互和参与关系来适应，并在序列学习任务中验证了该方法能在保持性能的同时减少能力修改。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经网络主要通过修改参数来适应，即使引入了模块化、条件计算和参数高效微调等方法，也未能将"计算能力"与"计算可访问性"区分为两个独立的适应变量，缺乏对计算关系层面自适应机制的探索。

Method: 提出可访问性可塑性原则，通过基于关系的操作实现形式化，建立"重用优先"适应层级（可访问性修改优先于更昂贵的能力和结构变化），并在序列学习任务上进行概念验证评估。

Result: 可访问性适应能够在保持可比任务性能的同时，减少能力修改（即参数变更）的需求。

Conclusion: 可访问性是一个独立的自适应维度，为未来计算关系能随环境动态演化的神经网络系统奠定了基础。

Abstract: Modern neural networks primarily adapt through parameter modification within predefined computational structures. While recent methods introduce modularity, conditional computation, and parameter-efficient adaptation, they generally do not distinguish computational capability from computational accessibility as separate adaptive variables. This work introduces Accessibility Plasticity, a principle of adaptive computation in which systems adapt not only by changing what computation exists, but also by reorganizing which existing computations can interact and participate. We formalize Accessibility Plasticity through a relationship-based operational realization and establish a reuse-first hierarchy of adaptation, where accessibility modification precedes more costly capability and structural changes. A proof-of-concept evaluation on sequential learning tasks shows that accessibility adaptation can reduce capability modification while maintaining comparable task performance. These results suggest accessibility as a distinct adaptive dimension and provide a foundation for future dynamic neural systems whose computational relationships evolve with changing environments.

</details>


### [227] [Hierarchical Grading in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22757)
*T. Shaska*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出分级大语言模型（GLLMs），通过代数框架为Transformer表示空间赋予分级结构，利用几何不变量理论优化分级方向，证明最优分级可通过凸规划在训练前确定，且训练后GLLM可转化为标准Transformer，不增加推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Transformer架构缺乏对表示空间的结构化分级，而自然语言数据往往具有层次性（如词、短语、句子等层级）。作者希望通过引入分级代数框架，使模型能够自动适应数据的分层结构，同时保持标准Transformer的表达能力和计算效率。

Method: 1) 构建GLLMs代数框架，在Transformer嵌入、自注意力和训练目标中引入分级加权标量作用；2) 利用几何不变理论，通过Kempf-Ness泛函和Hilbert-Mumford准则刻画最优分级方向；3) 针对层级分层目标，证明分级先验与无分级先验之间的极小极大分离界。

Result: 1) 最优分级方向构成开凸锥，可通过凸规划离线求解；2) 在层级分层数据上，分级模型与均匀模型的风险分离因子随层数指数级衰减；3) 训练后GLLM可编译为标准Transformer，不改变架构和推理复杂度。

Conclusion: GLLMs将Transformer推广为更大的分级家族，标准Transformer只是该家族边界上的一个特例（半稳定各向同性点）。该方法在不增加推理开销的前提下，利用数据的分层结构提升统计效率，为预训练架构选择提供了理论指导。

Abstract: We introduce Graded Large Language Models (GLLMs), an algebraic framework that equips the representation space of a transformer with a grading and propagates the induced weighted scalar action through embeddings, self-attention, and the training objective. The construction extends the theory of graded neural networks and graded transformers to autoregressive language models while preserving expressive power, asymptotic computational complexity, and inference cost.
  The governing geometric picture is that of geometric invariant theory. The benefit of a grading is expressed by a Kempf--Ness functional on the grading torus; the grades that improve upon the uniform architecture form an open convex cone whose membership is decided by a Hilbert--Mumford-type criterion pairing a grade direction against two measurable profiles of the target and the data; the optimal grades are the coincidence point of two moment maps, given in closed form; and the ordinary transformer appears as a semistable isotropic point on the boundary of the cone: one member of a larger graded family rather than a distinguished optimum.
  Separately, for level-stratified targets we prove a minimax separation between the graded prior and its absence: over all estimators the risks of the graded and uniform target classes separate throughout an explicit window of sample sizes, by a factor that decays exponentially in the number of levels under geometric stratification. Both profiles are estimable offline, so the optimal grades solve a convex program certified before training begins. Because the grading is absorbed into the learned parameters after training, every GLLM compiles to a standard transformer of identical architecture and inference complexity.

</details>


### [228] [An Integrated Deep Learning and Statistical Framework for Whole-Network Gene--Environment Association with Leaf Vascular Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22763)
*Geran Zhao,Yangsheng Wang,Xiaotian Dai,Guifang Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种集成深度学习与统计的框架，从RGB图像中完整提取叶片网络结构表型，并通过半参数稀疏典型相关分析识别与叶片脉管结构相关的基因-地理交互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基因-环境关联研究仅使用少量低维摘要性状量化叶脉结构，丢弃了原始图像中的大部分结构信息。

Method: 1) 以全网络图像表型表示叶片维管结构；2) 微调EDTER模型提取完整叶片脉管网络；3) 结合DiffusionEdge与BSDS500构建新注释数据集；4) 应用SSCCA进行高维双变量图像响应与高维预测变量的关联建模。

Result: 两项仿真实验验证框架性能，真实杨树数据集识别出三个与叶片脉管结构相关的显著基因-地理交互作用。

Conclusion: 提供了新的生物学见解，并建立了适用于高维复杂图像表型的通用方法框架。

Abstract: Leaf veins exhibit remarkable diversity in architecture and patterning, yet existing gene--environment association studies have primarily quantified leaf venation using a small collection of low-dimensional summary traits, thereby discarding most of the structural information contained in the original images. We propose an integrated deep learning and statistical framework. The proposed framework achieves four methodological advances. First, it represents the complete leaf vascular architecture as a whole-network image phenotype. Second, it fine-tunes the deep learning-based Edge Detection with Transformers (EDTER) model to accurately extract whole-network leaf vascular architecture from RGB images by jointly learning local and global contextual features. Third, it constructs a new annotated leaf image database by integrating edge maps generated by DiffusionEdge with the Berkeley Segmentation Database (BSDS500). Fourth, it applies Semiparametric Sparse Canonical Correlation Analysis (SSCCA) to perform variable selection and model associations between repeatedly measured high-dimensional Bivariate image responses and high-dimensional predictors while simultaneously accommodating sparse, zero-inflated data represented by edge maps through a truncated latent Gaussian copula model. Two simulation studies demonstrate the performance of the proposed framework under increasing levels of complexity. Application to a real \emph{Populus} dataset identifies three significant gene--geography interactions associated with leaf vascular architecture, providing new biological insights and establishing a broadly applicable methodological framework for high-dimensional complex image phenotypes.

</details>


### [229] [Beyond Shapley: An Influence-Based Data Auditing Pipeline for LLM Alignment and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22766)
*Yunting Song,Matthew Watson,Peter Grabowski,Jun Qin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Shapley值近似的可扩展数据估值管道，通过语义k-NN有向图评估数据效用，无需迭代重训练即可识别对齐数据集中的标签错误和安全隐患。在HelpSteer2和HH-RLHF数据集上验证，该方法将人工审计空间缩减99.1%，并发现了数千条隐藏的安全偏好反转样本，揭示了现有基准评估的严重漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLM）的对齐效果日益受限于数据质量。随着数据集规模扩大，海量偏好和指令微调语料中不可避免地积累了结构性矛盾、安全风险和系统性人工标注错误。现有审计方法（如语义去重或LLM-as-a-judge）难以捕捉单个样本对模型预测的实际影响，且常遗漏深层功能规则冲突。

Method: 提出一个可扩展、仅需推理的数据估值管道，近似计算Shapley值而无需迭代重训练。通过将语义k-NN邻域映射为有向图，利用参考LLM的零样本和单样本条件对数似然偏移直接评估数据效用，并将预测影响分数转化为局部优势指标，以隔离梯度冲突的记录。

Result: 在HelpSteer2数据集上，人工审计搜索空间减少99.1%，成功发现多种失败模式下的错误标签记录。在Anthropic的HH-RLHF数据集中，识别出数千条隐藏的安全和事实偏好反转。对评估分区的审计进一步暴露了当前基准评估的严重脆弱性，即能力强的模型预测更安全或更有用的回答，却被客观上错误的人工标注惩罚。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个数学基础扎实、高效的数据诊断工具，能够发现人工标签错误、净化评估基准，并确保LLM对齐数据的完整性。

Abstract: The alignment of Large Language Models (LLMs) is increasingly bottlenecked by data quality. As datasets scale, massive preference and instruction-tuning corpora inevitably accumulate hidden structural contradictions, safety risks, and systemic human annotation errors. Standard dataset auditing methods, such as semantic deduplication or LLM-as-a-judge, struggle to capture the actual predictive impact of individual records and often miss deep functional rule clashes. To address this, we introduce a scalable, inference-only data valuation pipeline that approximates the Shapley value without iterative model retraining. By mapping semantic k-NN neighborhoods into a directed graph, our framework evaluates data utility directly through a reference LLM's probability distribution using zero-shot and one-shot conditional log-likelihood shifts. Our pipeline then translates these predictive influence scores into localized advantage metrics to isolate gradient-conflicting records. We demonstrate the pipeline's efficacy in sanitizing two heavily vetted alignment datasets. First, applying our pipeline to the HelpSteer2 dataset reduced the manual audit search space by 99.1%, successfully uncovering falsely-labeled records across diverse failure modes. Second, applying our automated audit strategy to Anthropic's HH-RLHF training and evaluation splits identified thousands of hidden safety and factual preference inversions. Crucially, by extending this audit to the evaluation split, we expose severe vulnerabilities in current benchmark integrity: highly capable models frequently predict the safer or more helpful response, only to be penalized by objectively flawed human ground-truth labels. Overall, our work provides a mathematically grounded, highly efficient diagnostic tool to uncover human label failures, sanitize evaluation benchmarks, and ensure the integrity of LLM alignment data.

</details>


### [230] [DomainPilot: Domain-Level Loss-Guided Two-Stage Data Mixture Optimization for Efficient Language Model Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22769)
*He Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DomainPilot 提出了一种基于领域级损失引导的两阶段数据混合优化框架，通过 Token 级领域损失监控和 Scaling Law/Mixing Law 指导的优化，在不增加数据量和训练成本的情况下，显著提升 LLM 在多个基准上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态数据调度方法在工业级预训练和 SFT 中存在 O(N) 成本过高、I/O 瓶颈、依赖辅助参考模型或损失信号混淆噪声、难度和 novelty 等问题，亟需一种轻量级且有效的混合优化方案。

Method: 提出 DomainPilot 框架：(1) Token 级领域损失监控，在不中断数据管道的情况下捕获领域学习动态；(2) Scaling Law 指导的粗优化阶段，拟合领域收敛曲线得到先验混合比例；(3) Mixing Law 指导的细优化阶段，通过受控扫掠实验建模跨领域交互效应。通过约 30 行的 Patch 架构集成到现有训练框架中。

Result: 在 Qwen3-1.7B 模型 SFT 上验证，优化后混合在 MMLU-Redux (+2%)、AIME24 (+1.8%)、LiveCodeBench v5 (+3.8%)、BFCL v3 (+3.6%) 上均取得提升，且未增加数据总量或训练成本。

Conclusion: 领域级训练信号可作为数据选择或辅助模型训练的高效轻量级替代方案，用于数据混合优化。

Abstract: The training efficacy of large language models (LLMs) is fundamentally constrained by the quality and composition of training data. Existing dynamic data scheduling methods face critical limitations in industrial-scale pretraining and supervised fine-tuning (SFT): data selection incurs prohibitive O(N) costs on terabyte-scale corpora, mixture optimization schemes introduce severe I/O bottlenecks or require training auxiliary reference models, and sample-level reweighting strategies rely on loss signals that conflate noise, difficulty, and novelty.
  We present DomainPilot, a domain-level loss-guided two-stage data mixture optimization framework. DomainPilot introduces token-level domain loss monitoring to capture per-domain learning dynamics during training without halting the data pipeline. Building on these signals, we propose a Scaling Law guided coarse optimization stage that fits domain-specific convergence curves and derives a principled prior for mixture adjustment. A subsequent Mixing Law guided fine optimization stage refines the mixture by modeling cross-domain interaction effects through controlled sweep experiments. The entire mechanism is realized via a patch-based architecture that injects domain-aware loss computation into existing training frameworks (e.g., MindSpeed/Megatron-LM) with only ~30 lines of framework-specific adapter code.
  We validate DomainPilot on the Qwen3-1.7B model during SFT. Compared to the original data mixture, our optimized mixture achieves improvements of +2% on MMLU-Redux, +1.8% on AIME24, +3.8% on LiveCodeBench v5, and +3.6% on BFCL v3, without increasing total data volume or training cost. These results demonstrate that domain-level training signals provide an effective, lightweight alternative to expensive data selection or auxiliary model training for mixture optimization.

</details>


### [231] [Dementia Etiology Diagnosis via Collaborative Meta Knowledge Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22770)
*Siyuan Du,Mengxi Chen,Xinyang Jiang,Zilong Wang,Jiangchao Yao,Dongsheng Li,Ya Zhang,Lili Qiu,Yanfeng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出COME框架，通过注入多中心异质性感知嵌入和信任域约束优化，在7个独立队列上实现痴呆病因诊断SOTA性能（平均宏AUC 85.62%），并具备跨中心和跨序列的优异泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI方法在痴呆病因诊断中面临症状重叠的挑战，且跨中心数据存在异质性，传统多任务学习未能利用关键元信息（如中心采集参数、模态可用性）来应对这一问题。

Method: 提出COME框架：1) 将多中心采集语义、源标识符和模态指标作为异质性感知嵌入注入统一Transformer架构；2) 设计信任域约束优化方案，通过参考模型正则化训练，避免虚假相关性。

Result: 在7个独立队列上达到SOTA，平均宏AUC为85.62%，比最强基线高出4.29分；在跨中心和跨序列评估中均保持优越的域外泛化能力；模型预测与淀粉样蛋白、tau等生物标志物及临床严重程度高度一致。

Conclusion: COME框架能够实现鲁棒且可解释的痴呆诊断，具备在真实世界临床场景中应用的潜力。

Abstract: Although artificial intelligence (AI) has shown promising performance in several medical tasks, accurate dementia etiology diagnosis with AI remains challenging due to complex overlapping symptoms among diseases. Scaling up the dataset size by combining the cross-center samples may bring a gain in the pursuit of performance, while the inherent data heterogeneity across centers or populations induces the conflict. Conventional multi-task learning paradigms offer a promising framework; however, they fail to consider critical meta information (e.g., site-specific acquisition and modality availability) to combat the heterogeneity. To address this challenge, we propose a Collaborative Meta Knowledge Enhancement (COME) framework for dementia etiology diagnosis, which injects multi-center acquisition semantics, source identifiers, and modality indicators as heterogeneity-aware embeddings into a unified Transformer architecture for scale-up training, enabling explicit modeling of heterogeneity. Besides, a trust-region constrained optimization scheme is designed to regularize the model from spurious correlations during training through a reference model. Across seven independent cohorts, our method achieves state-of-the-art in-domain performance with a mean macro-averaged AUC of 85.62% and a 4.29-point gain over the strongest baseline, while maintaining superior out-of-domain generalization under both cross-center and cross-sequence evaluations. Extensive validation also confirms the alignment between model predictions and established biomarkers (amyloid, tau) and clinical severity, highlighting the potential of COME to enable robust and interpretable dementia diagnostics in real-world settings.

</details>


### [232] [CC-AOS: Cost- and Horizon-Conditioned Amortized Backward Induction for Finite-Horizon Optimal Stopping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22774)
*Tianwei Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CC-AOS，一种基于成本与水平条件化的摊销最优停止求解器，通过共享延续价值模型和结构化归纳偏置，实现对连续成本和多水平有限时域最优停止问题的高效求解，在FordA基准上相比独立拟合方法平均降低15.75%的损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动逆向归纳方法需为每个成本-水平工作点独立求解和存储模型，导致连续成本适应和多水平部署效率低下。

Method: 提出CC-AOS，学习一个共享的延续价值模型，该模型以当前状态、绝对时间、剩余水平和获取成本为条件，通过联合摊销拟合逆向归纳进行训练；并在模型架构中编码价值函数关于成本的非递减、凹、水平相关Lipschitz等理论性质。

Result: 在6个未见过的FordA成本-水平对上，单个CC-AOS检查点全部优于独立拟合的凸函数学习法，平均损失降低15.75%，且平均匹配调优静态阈值。

Conclusion: CC-AOS通过结构化摊销框架和理论驱动的归纳偏置，有效解决了多水平、连续成本下的最优停止问题，提升了泛化能力和部署效率。

Abstract: Finite-horizon optimal stopping is a central problem in early time-series classification, where a system must decide at each sequence prefix whether the expected benefit of another observation justifies its acquisition cost. Existing data-driven backward-induction methods typically solve each cost-horizon operating point separately, so changing operating conditions requires repeated optimization and separate model stacks, making continuous cost adaptation and multi-horizon deployment inefficient. We propose CC-AOS (Cost- and Horizon-Conditioned Amortized Optimal Stopping), a structured amortized solver for a family of finite-horizon stopping problems with continuous costs and multiple horizons. CC-AOS learns a shared continuation-value model conditioned on the current state, absolute time, remaining horizon, and acquisition cost through joint amortized fitted backward induction. We establish that the exact value and continuation functions are nondecreasing, concave, and horizon-dependently Lipschitz in cost, encode these properties in the model architecture, and derive residual-based bounds on value and policy errors. Experiments on controlled Gaussian and time-varying non-Gaussian processes and the FordA engine-noise time-series benchmark compare CC-AOS with representative per-operating-point backward-induction solvers and tuned static stopping rules. At six unseen FordA cost-horizon pairs, one CC-AOS checkpoint achieved a lower terminal-risk-plus-sampling-cost objective than independently fitted Convex Function Learning at all six pairs, with an average reduction of 15.75 percent, while matching the tuned static thresholds on average.

</details>


### [233] [Predicting the Outcome of rTMS Depression Therapy using EEG Signals and CNN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22776)
*Wael Korani,Md Fahimul Kabir Chowdhury,Sadam AlQadi,Priyan Malarvizhi kumar,Reza Rostami,Reza Kazemi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出结合FBSE-ED时频图像表示与轻量级CNN模型，用于预测rTMS抑郁症疗法疗效，在私有数据集上达到93.60%的分类准确率，优于传统DWT方法和多种深度学习模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: rTMS是治疗重度抑郁症（MDD）的无创疗法，但需要一种能够提前预测治疗结果的方法，以支持更精准的临床决策。

Method: 使用FBSE-ED和DWT两种时频方法将EEG信号转化为图像，构建轻量级自定义CNN模型，采用10折交叉验证，并在两个私有rTMS数据库上进行训练和鲁棒性测试。

Result: FBSE-ED表示达到最高分类准确率93.60%，优于DWT；所提架构比EEG特定深度学习模型（EEGNet等）高3.62-10.72%，比预训练模型（Xception等）高23.03-27.35%。

Conclusion: 将先进信号分解与深度学习相结合，能够早期预测rTMS治疗反应，该框架可解释、计算高效，适合在现实精神科诊所部署。

Abstract: Repetitive transcranial magnetic stimulation (rTMS) is a non invasive therapy for Major Depressive Disorder (MDD). In this study, we generate images using two time frequency methods to represent EEG signals: Fourier-Bessel Series Expansion with Euclidean Distance (FBSE-ED) and Discrete Wavelet Transform (DWT). We propose an efficient deep learning classifier to predict the outcome of rTMS depression therapy. In this study, we use a private rTMS databases to train a lightweight custom Convolutional Neural Network (CNN) using 10-fold cross validation strategy in order to avoid any bias in our results. The results show that the FBSE-ED representation achieves the highest classification accuracy of 93.60\%, outperforming traditional time-frequency technique (DWT). In addition, the proposed architecture with FBSE-ED image representation technique outperforms more complex EEG-Specific deep learning models (EEGNet, DeepConvNet, SleepEEGNet) by 3.62-10.72% and pretrained models (Xception, DenseNet201, and MobileNetV2) by 23.03-27.35%. For more experiments, we utilize another private rTMS database as test database to show the robustness of the proposed model. Our results suggest that integrating advanced signal decomposition with deep learning can facilitate early prediction of rTMS treatment response and support more targeted clinical decision-making. The proposed framework is interpretable, computationally efficient, and well-suited for deployment in real-world local psychiatric clinics.

</details>


### [234] [LC-SEPLM: long-range contact-supervised adaptation for sequence-only protein representation learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22777)
*Chen Wang,Boming Kang,Qinghua Cui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LC-SEPLM在ESM2基础上引入长程残基对接触监督，提升了蛋白质序列表示能力，在远程同源性识别等任务上取得显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蛋白质语言模型（如ESM2）主要基于氨基酸序列的上下文依赖建模，其训练目标未显式约束模型学习折叠后形成的三维残基接触信息，导致模型缺乏对空间结构的理解。

Method: 在ESM2基础上引入LoRA适配和长程残基对接触监督，利用对特异性查询通过交叉注意力机制从完整序列中提取与长程空间接触相关的全局序列上下文，并在50万AlphaFold Swiss-Prot蛋白质上进行训练。

Result: 在所有8个蛋白质级任务上均优于ESM2，远程同源性识别任务macro-F1从0.6122提升至0.6769（+6.47%），在ESM-S EC基准上最大绝对增益达0.1771。

Conclusion: 残基对接触监督是在保持序列推理能力的同时，将结构信息引入蛋白质序列表示的有效且受限的途径。

Abstract: Protein language models learn transferable sequence representations. However, because they primarily model contextual dependencies along amino-acid sequences, their training objectives do not explicitly constrain the model to learn three-dimensional residue contacts formed after folding . Here, we introduce LC-SEPLM (Long-range Contact-supervised ESM Protein Language Model), which adapts ESM2 with LoRA and long-range residue-pair contact supervision while retaining sequence-only downstream inference. Pair-specific queries use cross-attention over the complete sequence to extract global sequence context associated with long-range spatial contacts. To expose the model to diverse structural information, we trained LC-SEPLM on 500,000 AlphaFold Swiss-Prot proteins. In downstream evaluation, LC-SEPLM improved all eight protein-level tasks relative to ESM2. The largest gain occurred in remote-homology recognition, where macro-F1 increased from 0.6122 to 0.6769 (+0.0647, or 6.47 percentage points). On the official ESM-S EC benchmark, LC-SEPLM also outperformed ESM-S with a maximum absolute gain of 0.1771. These results support residue-pair contact supervision as a bounded route for introducing structural information into protein sequence representations while preserving sequence-only inference.

</details>


### [235] [Multimodal Surface EMG Hand Gesture Recognition Using Query-Based Transformers for Prosthetic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22779)
*Federico Del Pup,Elisa Tentori,Manfredo Atzori*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EMG-CrossFormer，一种混合卷积-Transformer架构，用于多模态融合的手势识别。该模型通过级联交叉注意力融合层整合多种生理信号（sEMG、惯性数据等），在四个NinaPro数据集上显著优于现有方法，多模态融合后准确率最高提升至92.79%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习模型在手势数量增加时性能下降，主要因为：1）纯卷积架构无法捕捉长程时序依赖；2）单模态设置无法利用多源生理信号的互补信息。因此需要一种能同时建模局部-全局特征并支持多模态融合的架构。

Method: 提出EMG-CrossFormer，包含：1）多个单模态编码器分别处理不同生理信号；2）级联交叉注意力融合层整合多模态特征；3）可学习的姿态查询向量解码融合后的表示，实现端到端的多模态手势识别。

Result: 在四个NinaPro数据集上，纯sEMG模式准确率72.33%-79.16%；融合惯性信号后提升至80.40%-92.79%，大幅超越六种SOTA模型。实验证明局部-全局联合建模与多模态融合均显著有效。

Conclusion: 局部-全局特征联合建模能有效提升sEMG手势识别的性能，而多模态融合进一步放大了这一优势，两种设计原则对于复杂手势识别任务都具有重要价值。

Abstract: Hand gesture recognition via surface electromyography (sEMG) is fundamental to prosthetic control. In this field, deep learning approaches have become the gold standard. However, current architectures struggle to scale; model performance typically decreases as the number of hand movements increases. Performance degradation is tied to the increased statistical complexity of decoding expanded gesture sets and compounded by the limitations of state-of-the-art methods, which primarily rely on low-latency unimodal convolutional architectures. Convolutions operate locally, limiting model's ability to capture long-range sequential patterns. Unimodal setups cannot leverage complementary information from coordinated signals characterizing movement execution, such as inertial and eye-tracking data. These limitations motivate architectures that integrate local and global features across multimodal physiological sequences. To bridge this gap, this study introduces EMG-CrossFormer, an end-to-end hybrid convolutional-transformer for seamless multimodal integration. EMG-CrossFormer combines representations from an arbitrary number of unimodal encoders through cascaded cross-attention fusion layers, and decodes the fused representations using learnable gesture queries. EMG-CrossFormer was evaluated on four NinaPro datasets (DB2, DB3, DB7, and DB10) and benchmarked against six state-of-the-art models using an increasing number of modalities. Using only sEMG, EMG-CrossFormer achieved mean accuracies of 72.33%, 52.48%, 79.16%, and 73.49% on DB2, DB3, DB7, and DB10, respectively. Incorporating inertial signals improved performance to 90.66%, 80.40%, 92.79%, and 92.06%. These results show that joint local-global feature modeling improves sEMG-only decoding and that multimodal fusion substantially amplifies this benefit, underscoring the value of both design principles for complex hand gesture recognition.

</details>


### [236] [What Softmax Throws Away: Mass-Aware Attention for Evidence Accumulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22781)
*Minwoo Yu,Young-guk Ha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出质量感知注意力(MAA)，通过Lp范数归一化解决标准注意力中重复证据导致的表示信息丢失问题，在不增加参数和监督的情况下提升图模型表示的信息含量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准注意力机制对重复证据模式不敏感，导致模型内部表示丢失了预测相关的结构信息，即使任务性能高，也无法保证表示保留了足够的信息量。

Method: 提出Mass-Aware Attention (MAA)，将标准L1归一化推广到Lp族，使重复输入时分子分母以不同速率缩放，从而在表示幅度中保留有效贡献输入的数量信息，p=1时还原为标准注意力。

Result: 在4个连续时间动态图模型和3个数据集上，MAA在11/12的模型-数据集中提升未来链接AUC；线性恢复提升4.49%；偏好依附恢复在12/12中改善；在MTPP、TKG、RAG、STPP中均观察到一致证据。

Conclusion: MAA是一种通用的归一化原则，通过控制标准注意力中的重复不变性来提升面向预测器的表示信息含量，且信息可访问性与任务效用是分离的。

Abstract: High task performance does not show whether a model retains prediction-relevant structural information in its internal representation. Temporal graph models, for example, can achieve high future-link AUC while basic graph statistics remain difficult to recover from the same representation. We identify one source of this gap in the weighted averaging used by standard attention: when an evidence pattern is repeated, the numerator and denominator grow at the same rate, so inputs with different amounts of accumulated evidence can produce the same aggregate.
  We propose Mass-Aware Attention (MAA), which generalizes standard L1 normalization to an Lp family. Under repetition, MAA makes the numerator and denominator scale at different rates, retaining the effective number of contributing inputs in the representation magnitude. It adds no supervision, parameters, hidden dimensions, or explicit count features, and recovers standard attention at p=1.
  Across four continuous-time dynamic graph models and three datasets, MAA improves future-link AUC in 11 of 12 model-dataset cells. Linear recovery from the same hidden representation increases by 4.49% on average, and preferential-attachment recovery improves in all 12 cells after family-wise correction. We also observe consistent evidence in marked temporal point processes, temporal knowledge graphs, retrieval-augmented generation, and spatio-temporal point processes. Information accessibility and task utility remain distinct: NLL improves in MTPP, ranking is largely preserved in TKG, additional information in RAG does not improve the diagnostic head, and downstream LayerNorm can erase the signal in STPP. These results position MAA as a general normalization principle for improving predictor-facing representation informativeness by controlling repetition invariance in standard attention.

</details>


### [237] [Optimizing Transformer Neural Network for Real-Time Outlier Detection on FPGAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22786)
*Ilia Sobakinskikh,Paul Alexander Bilokon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探索了利用FPGA硬件加速Transformer神经网络推理时间，以实现金融时间序列的实时异常检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融时间序列数据存在异常值和数据量激增的问题，现有数据清洗方法在精度和处理速度上难以满足实时异常检测需求。

Method: 研究多种Transformer架构进行时间序列建模，并将其高效部署在PYNQ-Z2 FPGA开发板上，以最小化推理延迟。

Result: 成功在FPGA上实现了Transformer的加速推理，有效降低了异常检测的延迟。

Conclusion: FPGA硬件加速结合Transformer架构能够有效提升金融时间序列实时异常检测的效率，代码已开源。

Abstract: In this work, we explore how the inference time of a Transformer Neural Network can be efficiently optimized with applications to real-time anomaly detection in financial time series. The financial time series are price series such as asset prices. Unfortunately, the data is often with errors or outliers that make the downstream data processing tasks useless, unstable or even harmful. Moreover, the amount of financial time-series data has been significantly increasing. Hence, there is a need for better data-cleaning methods in terms of accuracy and in terms of processing speed.
  Transformers as a neural network architecture have achieved superior performances in many tasks such as Natural Language Processing and Computer Vision. Time series modelling and especially anomaly detection tasks can benefit from the features of transformers architecture in multiple ways, including the capacity to capture long-range dependencies and interactions.
  Increasingly powerful hardware, such as field-programmable gate arrays (FPGAs), have seen increasing usage in recent years due to their reconfigurability and high performance. They can be efficiently utilized to speed up the computations of the Transformer architecture.
  We explore different Transformer architectures for time series modelling and how they can be efficiently implemented on an FPGA board (PYNQ-Z2). In particular, we examine the application of Transformers to detect anomalies in time series and we show how they can be efficiently implemented on an FPGA board to minimize latency.
  The code is available at https://github.com/thxi/icl_thesis

</details>


### [238] [FMOPF: Latent Flow Matching with Constraint-Aware Interaction Priors for AC Optimal Power Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22788)
*Zhilin Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FMOPF框架，通过解耦压缩与生成（潜在流匹配）和显式建模负载-状态耦合（约束感知交互先验网络），解决了扩散模型在最优潮流问题中生成可行近优解分布时规模难扩展、解质量差的问题，在IEEE测试系统上实现了最优的牛顿-拉夫逊热启动、最低尾部风险，并首次扩展到数百节点系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着可再生能源渗透率加深，单一最优调度方案不再足够，运营商需要可行近优解的分布表征以进行风险量化、灵敏度分析和多目标权衡评估。现有监督神经网络只能提供点预测，扩散模型在原始状态空间操作时解质量下降且无法扩展到中等以上规模系统。

Method: 提出FMOPF框架，核心思想是通过潜在流匹配（latent flow matching）将高维OPF解流形的压缩（compression）与条件生成（generation）解耦，并设计约束感知交互先验网络（Constraint-Aware Interaction Prior Network）显式建模负载与状态之间的耦合关系。

Result: 在四个IEEE测试系统上的实验表明：FMOPF提供最有效的牛顿-拉夫逊热启动，在生成方法中实现最低尾部风险，是首个能扩展到数百节点系统且保持完全可行性的生成方法。消融研究验证了潜在生成管道是物理可行性的必要条件，交互先验充当了后期尾部风险控制器。

Conclusion: FMOPF通过解耦压缩与生成、显式建模负载-状态耦合，有效解决了现有生成模型在OPF问题中规模扩展性和解质量的瓶颈，为电力系统运行提供了高质量多样化的可行近优解分布。

Abstract: AC optimal power flow determines the minimum-cost generation dispatch under nonlinear power balance constraints and is solved thousands of times daily in electricity market operations. Learning a direct mapping from load conditions to OPF solutions can accelerate this computation, yet with deepening renewable penetration, a single optimal dispatch is no longer sufficient. Operators require a characterization of the distribution of feasible near-optimal solutions for risk quantification, sensitivity analysis, and multi-objective trade-off assessment. Supervised neural networks provide fast point predictions but cannot capture this conditional distribution. Diffusion-based generative models can sample diverse solutions in principle, yet existing methods operating in the raw state space exhibit degraded solution quality and fail to scale beyond medium-sized systems. We identify the root cause as the conflation of two distinct tasks within a single model. Compressing the high-dimensional OPF solution manifold is one task, and learning the conditional mapping from loads to that manifold is another. This paper presents FMOPF, a framework that resolves this conflation by decoupling compression from generation through latent flow matching and by explicitly modeling load-state coupling through a Constraint-Aware Interaction Prior Network. Experiments on four IEEE test systems demonstrate that FMOPF provides the most effective Newton-Raphson warm starts, achieves the lowest tail risk among generative methods, and is the first such method to scale to systems with several hundred buses while preserving full feasibility. Ablation studies confirm that the latent generation pipeline is a necessary condition for physical feasibility and that the interaction prior functions as a late-stage tail-risk controller.

</details>


### [239] [Multimodal Domain Generalization for Depression Detection: An Attention-Based BiLSTM Network with Domain-Adversarial Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22794)
*Ali Tabaraei,Federico Simonetta,Stavros Ntalampiras*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出首个患者独立的多模态抑郁检测框架，通过领域泛化（DG）结合音频和文本模态，利用BiLSTM、注意力机制和梯度反转层提升跨说话人的泛化能力，在Androids-Corpus数据集上达到93.2%的准确率和94.2%的F1分数，超越现有标杆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于深度学习的自动抑郁检测方法因说话人间差异导致的领域偏移问题，泛化能力有限。为克服这一关键问题，作者提出首个患者独立的多模态抑郁检测框架，引入领域泛化技术。

Method: 采用BiLSTM结合模态内和跨模态注意力机制，通过分段级融合进行决策；引入基于DANN的梯度反转层，以对抗性方式限制模型识别个体说话人的能力，从而学习领域不变表征，减少患者特定偏差。

Result: 在Androids-Corpus数据集上采用5折交叉验证，确定MelSpec和ItalianBERT在30秒分段时长下为最优基线。加入DG后准确率提升2.5%，F1分数提升3.3%，最终达到93.2%准确率、93.2%精确率、96.2%召回率、94.2%F1分数，超越所有现有基准。

Conclusion: 消融研究验证了多模态融合、深度架构选择和领域泛化的综合贡献，表明该方法能有效实现鲁棒且可泛化的抑郁检测。

Abstract: Automatic depression detection with deep learning has shown promise but often suffers from limited generalization due to domain shift arising from inter-speaker variability. To address this critical issue, we present the first patient-independent multimodal depression detection framework that incorporates domain generalization (DG), jointly leveraging both acoustic and textual modalities. The proposed model integrates bidirectional Long Short-Term Memory (BiLSTM) with intra- and cross-modal attention mechanisms, accompanied by segment-level fusion for decision-making. Generalization is further enhanced by applying a gradient reversal layer inspired by Domain-Adversarial Training of Neural Networks (DANN), which promotes domain-invariant representations by adversarially limiting the model's ability to identify individual speakers, effectively reducing patient-specific bias. Conducting experiments on the Androids-Corpus dataset with a 5-fold cross-validation (CV) protocol, various pairings of audio and text feature extractors were evaluated over different segment durations, determining MelSpec and ItalianBERT as the optimal baseline at a 30-second segment duration. The addition of DG to this baseline yields a 2.5% increase in accuracy and 3.3% in F1-score, achieving 93.2% accuracy, 93.2% precision, 96.2% recall, and 94.2% F1-score, surpassing all existing benchmarks. Extensive ablation studies assess the impact of multimodal fusion, deep architectural choices, and DG, highlighting their combined contribution to robust and generalizable depression detection.

</details>


### [240] [Physically Verifiable Evidence and LLM-Based Reporting for Bearing Fault Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22797)
*Yuntong Chen,Jianyu Liu,Guobin Zhao,Ziang Wang,Chao Chen,Ju Huang,Xitian Tian,Lijiang Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出诊断证据网络（DENet），通过输出可物理验证的结构化证据（特征频率、瞬态冲击定位）替代传统分类器仅输出置信度分数的做法，并约束语言模型仅翻译不生成诊断内容，从而提升AI诊断在安全关键机械系统中的可信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能故障诊断方法输出的是分类标签+softmax置信度分数，这种内部统计量无法与独立物理知识进行交叉验证；同时，生成式语言模型在维护报告中的应用可能导致幻觉内容混入决策依据中，存在安全风险。

Method: 提出DENet，一种编码器无关的多任务框架，扩展输出为结构化证据记录：分类结果、与实际理论值可比较的预测特征频率、以及可在原始波形上检查的瞬态冲击时间定位；同时采用QLoRA适配语言模型，约束其仅翻译而不生成诊断内容。

Result: 在四种编码器和三个公开数据集上，DENet无显著统计精度损失，频率误差约6Hz（1024点段上频谱估计不可用）；预测频率与理论频率的偏差作为无标签推理时验证信号，误检AUROC达0.970和0.871，在高置信度区间仍保持判别力；语言模型将无依据声明率从10-12%降至2%，消除虚构数量。

Conclusion: 通过输出可物理验证的结构化证据并约束语言模型行为，DENet有效提升了AI诊断的可信度和可验证性，为安全关键机械系统中AI的可信部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Trustworthy deployment of AI-based diagnosis in safety-critical mechanical systems hinges on validation: whether a prediction can be checked against physical reality before it is acted upon. Current intelligent fault diagnosers fail this standard in two ways. Their standard output, a class label with a softmax confidence score, is an internal statistic of the classifier, offering nothing checkable against independent physical knowledge; and the growing use of generative language models in maintenance reporting adds a second risk: hallucinated content entering reports on which decisions rest. Taking bearing fault diagnosis as the testbed, this work addresses both problems from the output side. The proposed Diagnostic Evidence Network (DENet) is an encoder-agnostic multi-task framework extending the output to a structured evidence record: the classification, a predicted characteristic frequency comparable against the theoretical value determined by bearing geometry and shaft speed, and a temporal localization of transient impulses inspectable on the raw waveform. Across four encoders and three public datasets, this evidence incurs no statistically significant accuracy cost, with a frequency error of about 6 Hz on 1,024-point segments where spectral estimation is structurally inapplicable. Centrally, the deviation between predicted and theoretical frequency constitutes a label-free, inference-time validation signal: it detects misclassifications with AUROC values of 0.970 and 0.871, and remains discriminative in the high-confidence regime where confidence-derived detectors are blind. Finally, a QLoRA-adapted language model is constrained to translate, but never generate, diagnostic content, reducing unsupported-claim rates from 10-12% to 2% and eliminating fabricated quantities.

</details>


### [241] [LithoFormer: A Robust Framework for Stratigraphic Inference via Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22804)
*Shwetha Salimath,Francesca Bugiotti,Sylvain Wlodarczyk,Sohaib Ouzineb*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LithoFormer是一种基于Seq2Seq Transformer的框架，通过一次性输入多变量测井数据，实现地质层序推断，相比传统滑动窗口方法，边界误差中位数降低90%，消除了地层顺序违规，并减少了80%的人工专家工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动地质表征技术主要采用滑动窗口分类方法，难以理解更广泛的地质上下文，导致地层分层错位，无法准确捕捉长距离地质依赖关系。

Method: 提出LithoFormer框架，使用Seq2Seq Transformer模型一次性输入完整的多变量测井数据；采用通道独立的PatchTST骨干网络，结合旋转位置编码（RoPE）捕捉长距离地质依赖；设计解耦多任务头联合预测地质分带和边界概率；引入地质信息损失函数强制执行地层叠覆律等物理约束。

Result: 在三个真实数据集上验证，边界误差中位数降低90%，消除地层顺序违规；减少80%的人工专家工作量，消除地层不一致性。

Conclusion: LithoFormer为大规模地下建模提供了可扩展且可靠的解决方案，显著提升了地质表征的准确性和效率。

Abstract: Accurate geological characterization of subsurface reservoirs from well log data is essential to support projects such as carbon capture and storage (CCS), geothermal development, and extraction of natural resources. Existing automated techniques for geological characterization primarily use sliding-window classification, which limits their ability to understand broader geological contexts, often leading to misaligned formation layers. To overcome these limitations, we introduce LithoFormer, a robust framework for stratigraphic inference using a Seq2Seq transformer model that ingests entire multivariate well logs in a single pass. The framework utilizes a channel-independent PatchTST backbone enhanced with rotary positional embeddings (RoPE) to capture long-range geological dependencies across entire multivariate well logs. A decoupled multi-task head is employed to jointly predict geological zonation and precise boundary probabilities, while a geology-informed loss function enforces physical constraints such as the Law of Superposition. Validated and deployed on three real-world datasets, LithoFormer demonstrates a 90% reduction in median boundary error and eliminates stratigraphic order violations compared to traditional sliding-window baselines. It also achieves a 80% reduction in manual expert labor and eliminates stratigraphic inconsistencies, providing a scalable and reliable solution for large-scale subsurface modeling.

</details>


### [242] [OrchNAS: Orchestrated Neural Architecture Search Service for Personalised Federated Edge Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22805)
*Keya Patel,Sajib Mistry,Sheik Mohammad Mostakim Fattah,Aneesh Krishna*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出OrchNAS框架，通过NAS服务自动设计适应异构边缘环境的能量感知、个性化联邦边缘智能模型，包含能量感知全局搜索、渐进式剪枝和原始-对偶优化机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决异构边缘环境下设备能量、计算和内存资源差异大，现有方法无法自动设计适应性模型的问题。

Method: 1) 服务器端NAS服务自动搜索架构；2) 能量感知全局架构搜索学习跨异构服务的紧凑全局表示；3) 渐进式贪婪能量感知剪枝策略获取个性化子网；4) 原始-对偶优化机制在架构适应过程中强制执行严格能量预算。

Result: 在真实世界和基准数据集上的实验验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: OrchNAS能有效为异构边缘环境自动设计能量感知的个性化模型，兼顾效率和资源约束。

Abstract: We propose OrchNAS, an energy-aware, personalised, federated edge intelligence framework that leverages a Neural Architecture Search Service to automatically design service-adaptive models for heterogeneous edge environments. The framework orchestrates the architecture search process on a server-side NAS service, enabling edge services to derive personalised architectures under device-level energy, computation, and memory constraints. We introduce an energy-aware global architecture search mechanism that learns a compact global representation across heterogeneous services. We develop an energy-efficient architecture selection mechanism that enables each service to derive a personalised subnet that satisfies its resource constraints via a progressive, greedy, energy-aware pruning strategy. We propose an energy-efficient personalised model optimisation scheme that updates service-adaptive parameters while preserving global representations, where a primal-dual optimisation mechanism enforces strict energy budgets during architecture adaptation. Experiments on real-world and benchmark datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [243] [SLA-Constrained Carbon-Aware Routing in Geo-Distributed Serverless Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22806)
*Anmol Chaudhary,Rahul Mishra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种SLA约束的碳感知无服务器路由策略，在5个AWS区域实测中实现最高46.8%碳减排，平均27.4%，且零SLA违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有路由机制仅考虑延迟而忽略电网碳排放强度差异，导致不必要的碳排放，尤其是在清洁能源区域处于可接受延迟范围内时。

Method: 将碳感知无服务器路由问题建模为跨地理分布云区域的约束优化问题，提出SLA约束的碳感知路由策略，在SLA可行区域内实现最优碳减排。

Result: 最高46.8%碳减排，平均27.4%碳减排，零SLA违规；路由开销低于总延迟的0.02%；扩展到12个区域后平均碳节约从27.4%提升至47.5%。

Conclusion: 云系统可以在不牺牲用户体验的前提下实现显著的碳节约，有助于实现SDG 13（气候行动）和SDG 7（经济适用的清洁能源）。

Abstract: Modern cloud deployments distribute applications across multiple geographic regions, yet standard routing mechanisms prioritize latency while ignoring the fluctuating carbon intensity of local power grids. Latency-driven routing incurs avoidable carbon emissions, particularly when cleaner regions are within acceptable latency bounds. The proposed model formulates the carbon-aware serverless routing problem as a constrained optimization over geo-distributed cloud regions and introduces an SLA-constrained carbon-aware routing policy that achieves optimal carbon reduction within the SLA-feasible region, evaluated using real carbon intensity measurements across 5 primary AWS deployments. Experimental results show that the proposed policy achieves up to 46.8% carbon reduction while maintaining zero SLA violations across all evaluated thresholds. The system reduces carbon by an average of 27.4% under mixed workloads, and the routing overhead is very low (less than 0.02% of total request latency). A scalability study across 12 AWS regions spanning 6 continents demonstrates that average carbon savings increase from 27.4% to 47.5% as routing flexibility expands under mixed workloads. The proposed work contributes to SDG 13 (Climate Action) and SDG 7 (Affordable and Clean Energy) by enabling low-carbon routing decisions. These results indicate that cloud systems can achieve significant carbon savings without compromising user experience.

</details>


### [244] [From Hybrid Mechanistic--Data-Driven Modeling Toward Neuro-Symbolic AI: What, Why, and How](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22811)
*Moein E. Samadi,Andreas Schuppert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Hybrid-to-NeSy (H2N)框架，将混合机理/数据驱动模型重构为神经符号(NeSy)接口，从而引入结构违规率(SVR)和信念离散度(BD)两个度量，分别评估学习模块对机理结构的尊重程度以及机理部分的认知不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合模型设计主要通过架构和训练损失来指定，缺乏统一的语义接口进行跨领域比较和验证，且对机理部分的认知不确定性关注不足。

Method: 将混合模型重构为神经符号(NeSy)接口实例：机理知识置于语言侧，学习模块置于信念侧，有效域和约束置于逻辑侧；由此推导出NeSy推理泛函和逻辑-信念分解，并从中定义SVR和BD两个度量。

Result: 在含标签噪声的二分类结构化混合模型案例中，SVR和BD越高的模型，在留出集上准确率的变异性越大；在结构分布偏移下，H2N能量化外推时的不确定性，而测试准确率只能事后反映偏移。

Conclusion: H2N框架为混合模型提供了统一的语义接口和不确定性量化方法，能更早检测分布偏移，优于仅依赖测试准确率的传统评估方式。

Abstract: Hybrid mechanistic/data-driven models, which combine first-principles with learned components, are increasingly used in process engineering and scientific machine learning. Common hybrid modeling designs are specified primarily through their architectures and training losses, which offers a limited basis for a shared semantic interface to compare or verify them across domains, with comparatively little attention paid to epistemic uncertainty in the mechanistic part.
  We bridge hybrid modeling and neuro-symbolic (NeSy) AI by reconstructing these designs as instances of NeSy interface. The resulting translation, Hybrid-to-NeSy (H2N), places mechanistic knowledge on the language side, learned modules on the belief side, and validity domains together with constraints on the logic side. For each design, H2N then yields an explicit NeSy inference functional and a logic-belief decomposition.
  From this decomposition we derive two metrics: structural violation rate (SVR), measuring whether the learned belief respects the mechanistic structure; and belief dispersion (BD), measuring how concentrated the learned plausibility is, serving as a hybrid model's epistemic uncertainty in its mechanistic part. We instantiate H2N on a case study of a structured hybrid model for binary classification under label noise and show that models with higher SVR and BD exhibit greater variability in held-out accuracy. Under structural distribution shift, H2N further quantifies a model's uncertainty during extrapolations, whereas test accuracy reveals the same shift only post hoc.

</details>


### [245] [MEMENTO: Memory-Guided Memetic Code-as-Policy Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22832)
*Alkis Sygkounas,Victor Aregbede,Amy Loutfi,Andreas Persson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MEMENTO提出了一种基于记忆引导的模因进化框架，用于将控制策略表示为可执行代码（code-as-policy），通过进化评估器、爬山搜索、宏观变异和交叉操作，在长周期具身任务中显著优于现有方法，并成功实现从仿真到真实机器人的迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长周期具身任务需要执行大量依赖动作才能观察到任务成功，现有基于LLM的进化方法主要通过独立生成变体并选择高表现候选，缺乏序列化的局部改进阶段，难以高效搜索策略空间。

Method: 提出MEMENTO框架：1) 演化出一个"rollout评估器"，将策略执行结果映射为标量适应度和结构化反馈指标；2) 采用记忆引导的爬山算法、宏观变异和交叉操作生成策略提案，由评估器的反馈指标进行条件约束；3) 形成单精英模因进化框架。

Result: 在Robosuite Franka Hanoi塔操作和AI2-THOR家庭交互两个长周期具身任务上，MEMENTO在任务成功率和泛化能力上均超过Eureka和REvolve基线；消融实验显示零样本生成和未进化评估器均无法解决任务；最终在真实Franka机器人上成功部署。

Conclusion: MEMENTO通过引入记忆引导的局部改进机制和可进化评估器，有效提升了代码即策略方法的搜索效率和性能，且能够实现仿真到真实环境的迁移。

Abstract: Long-horizon embodied tasks require policies that execute many dependent actions before task success can be observed. Representing policies as executable control pro- grams (code-as-policy) enables their decision logic to be inspected and revised after rollout evaluation. Revised programs can then be executed and compared by rollout performance, framing policy improvement as execution-guided program search. Evo- lutionary methods driven by large language models (LLMs) provide a natural mecha- nism for this search by generating variants and selecting high-performing candidates. However, existing approaches primarily select among independently generated vari- ants and lack a sequential local improvement phase. We introduce MEMENTO, a memory-guided single-elite memetic framework for code-as-policy evolution. ME- MENTO first evolves a rollout evaluator that maps policy rollouts to scalar fitness and structured feedback metrics. Fitness selects accepted candidates and the next elite, while feedback metrics condition policy proposals generated by memory-guided hill-climbing, macro-mutation, and crossover. We evaluate MEMENTO on two long- horizon embodied domains: Robosuite Franka Tower-of-Hanoi manipulation and AI2- THOR household interaction. MEMENTO outperforms Eureka and REvolve, adapted as code-as-policy evolutionary baselines, in task success and generalization to held- out Robosuite object configurations and unseen AI2-THOR scenes. Ablations show that zero-shot generation and unevolved evaluators fail to solve either domain, and that removing policy-search branches reduces performance. Finally, we deploy the best-evolved Robosuite policy on a physical Franka robot, demonstrating the feasibil- ity of sim-to-real transfer of the evolved code-as-policy. Code, prompts, and videos are available at: https://github.com/sygkounas/MEMENTO.

</details>


### [246] [Frustratingly Simple Black-Box Adaptation of Language Models via Logit Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22837)
*Ofek I. Cohen,Lior Shani,Aviv Rosenberg,Ankur Samanta,Tal Wagner,Yonathan Efroni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种轻量级语言模型适配方法：通过黑盒学习一个固定的logit偏置向量，在解码时直接加到每个token的logits上，无需修改模型权重或梯度，即可提升模型在特定任务上的性能，效果接近传统微调但参数量极少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多组织希望将语言模型适配到内部使用，以提升领域任务性能并解决隐私问题，但现有方法（微调开源模型或优化提示词）操作复杂、成本高，因此需要一种更轻量级的替代方案。

Method: 提出一种黑盒方法，基于KL正则化的强化学习目标，学习一个跨上下文固定的logit偏置向量，在每步解码时添加；推导了闭式逆概率估计器，通过rollout、奖励和token概率进行学习。

Result: 在数学和推理基准测试上，该方法相比基础模型有显著提升，且使用的可训练参数远少于传统微调方法。

Conclusion: 学习的logit偏置是一种轻量级的语言模型适配机制，适用于对模型访问权限要求最低的场景。

Abstract: Many organizations aim to adapt language models for internal use, both to improve performance on domain-specific tasks and to address privacy concerns around sensitive data. However, such adaptation remains non-trivial: it often requires operationally challenging fine-tuning of open-source models or ad hoc prompt optimization. We study a minimal alternative based on a simple API-level control: allowing users to bias the model's logits with a user-defined vector. We develop a black-box method for learning a single context-independent logit-bias vector, added at every decoding step, without modifying model weights or requiring gradients. Starting from a KL-regularized reinforcement learning (RL) objective, we characterize when such a fixed logit-bias vector can approximate the optimal prefix-dependent correction and derive a closed-form inverse-propensity estimator from rollouts, rewards, and token probabilities. Empirically, this simple decoding-time intervention improves over base models on mathematical and reasoning benchmarks while using far fewer trainable parameters than conventional fine-tuning. Our results suggest that learned logit bias is a lightweight mechanism for adapting language models under minimal access requirements.

</details>


### [247] [Same Predictions, Different Reasons: The Effect of Quantization on Model Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22872)
*Kazi Kamruzzaman Rabbi,Md. Zami Al Zunaed Farabe,M. Sohel Rahman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了静态后训练量化（PTQ）对五种经典CNN架构（VGG19、ResNet18、EfficientNet-B0、DenseNet161、MobileNetV2）在INT8和INT4精度下可解释性的影响，发现分类精度不能可靠反映可解释性稳定性，DenseNet161表现最佳，而EfficientNet-B0在INT8下尽管分类精度高，但输入级特征归因显著退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究已表明量化能保持分类精度，但量化是否保留模型内部推理机制（可解释性）尚不明确，需系统评估。

Method: 采用Grad-CAM（空间注意力分析）和LIME（输入级特征归因）双可解释性框架，结合皮尔逊相关系数、结构相似性指数、Top-20% IoU三种互补指标及删除/插入忠实度分析，对5种CNN架构在INT8/INT4精度下进行系统比较。

Result: 分类精度不能可靠反映可解释性稳定性；DenseNet161在不同精度下保持强特征一致性；EfficientNet-B0在INT8下虽空间注意力和分类精度高，但输入级特征归因显著退化。

Conclusion: 量化部署中架构选择与量化策略同等重要，对高可解释性要求的可信应用具有直接启示意义。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) has become a practical solution for deploying deep learning models on resource-constrained edge devices by compressing high-precision floating-point weights into low-precision representations without requiring retraining. Past research has demonstrated that quantization largely preserves classification accuracy; however, whether it also preserves the model's internal reasoning remains an open question. This study presents a systematic evaluation on how static PTQ affects the interpretability / explainability of five widely used CNN architectures: VGG19, ResNet18, EfficientNet-B0, DenseNet161, and MobileNetV2 at INT8 and INT4 precision. We employ a dual interpretability framework that combines Grad-CAM for spatial attention analysis with LIME for input-level feature attribution, and systematically compare full-precision and quantized models on two binary classification datasets. Interpretability is evaluated using three complementary metrics: the Pearson correlation coefficient, structural similarity index, and top-20% IoU to capture distributional and structural variations in model explanations, supplemented by deletion/insertion faithfulness analysis. The results show that classification accuracy is not a reliable indicator of interpretability stability under reduced precision. DenseNet161 maintains strong feature consistency across both precision levels, whereas EfficientNet-B0, despite achieving competitive spatial attention and classification accuracy at INT8 precision, exhibits a substantial degradation in input-level feature attribution. These findings have direct implications for the trustworthy deployment of quantized models in applications with high interpretability requirements, demonstrating that architecture selection is as important as the quantization strategy.

</details>


### [248] [Spatial Prediction of Soil Microplastics and Organic Matter Using Graph Attention Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22875)
*Anik Dev Nath,Md Al Amin,Bikash Kumar Paul*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出基于图注意力网络（GAT）的空间土壤预测方法，利用91个地理参考样本预测微塑料和有机质含量。模型表现良好（微塑料RMSE=625.06, R²=0.87；有机质RMSE=0.43, R²=0.91），但交叉验证显示泛化能力有限，主要受限于小样本量和稀疏图结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确估算土壤微塑料和有机质含量对评估生态系统健康和可持续土地利用至关重要，现有方法未能充分考虑样本间的空间依赖关系。

Method: 采用图注意力网络（GAT）构建两层架构，融合空间坐标、土壤属性和土地利用数据，建模91个地理参考样本间的空间依赖关系。

Result: 模型在微塑料预测上RMSE=625.06（R²=0.87），有机质预测上RMSE=0.43（R²=0.91），但交叉验证泛化能力有限。

Conclusion: GAT在空间土壤预测中具有潜力，但需要更密集的数据集和更好的图连接性来提升泛化性能。

Abstract: Accurate estimation of soil microplastics and organic matter is essential to assess ecosystem health and support sustainable land use. This study presents a graph-based deep learning approach using Graph Attention Networks (GATs) to model spatial dependencies among 91 georeferenced soil samples. By incorporating spatial coordinates, soil properties, and land use data, a two-layer GAT architecture was developed to capture local interactions. The final model showed strong performance, achieving RMSEs of 625.06 ($R^2 = 0.87$) for microplastics and 0.43 ($R^2 = 0.91$) for organic matter. However, cross-validation results revealed limited generalization, probably due to the small sample size and sparse graph structure. These findings demonstrate the potential of GATs for spatial soil prediction and underscore the need for dense datasets and improved graph connectivity.

</details>


### [249] [Efficient Learning of Truncated Boolean Product Distributions: Influence to the Rescue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22889)
*Rohan Chauhan,Ioannis Panageas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了从被截断的布尔乘积分布中高效估计自然参数的问题，通过分析集合S在测度μ_z下的几何结构，改进了现有方法的样本复杂度，并推广了"fatness"条件，同时证明了样本复杂度在模型宽度和集合最小距离上存在指数依赖下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在估计截断布尔乘积分布的自然参数时存在局限：要么要求强局部连通性（fatness），要么要求严格的反集中性假设，且截断集总质量需为常数；当截断集质量指数小时，样本复杂度呈指数增长。本文旨在克服这些限制。

Method: 分析集合S在测度μ_z下的几何结构；利用布尔函数分析中的"影响力"概念推广fatness条件；提出无需在任意参数化下采样的新方法。

Result: 在fatness假设下将样本复杂度改进至O(log n / ε²)，达到未截断时的极小极大最优率；给出了基于影响力概念的推广条件，保证了高效推断；建立了样本复杂度关于模型宽度和集合最小距离的指数依赖下界。

Conclusion: 本文从几何角度分析了截断布尔分布中的参数估计问题，显著改进了现有方法的样本复杂度，并通过推广条件扩大了可高效推断的场景，同时揭示了该问题固有的指数复杂度障碍。

Abstract: Learning the natural parameters $z \in \mathbb{R}^n$ of discrete distributions $μ_z$ from independent samples constrained to a subset $S \subseteq \{0,1\}^n$ is a foundational challenge in high-dimensional statistics. Existing methods for efficiently estimating truncated Boolean product distributions, notably the work of [Fotakis et al' COLT'20, Algorithmica '22], require either strong local connectivity assumptions on $S$ -- a property denoted fatness -- or stringent anti-concentration assumptions and necessitate the total mass of the truncation set to be a constant with respect to $n$. Moreover, the results in [Fotakis et al' COLT'20, Algorithmica '22] suffer from sample complexities that scale as $Ω(2^n)$ if the mass of $S$ is exponentially small in $n$.
  In this work, we circumvent these limitations by analyzing the geometry of $S$ under the measure $μ_z$. We refine the existing parameter estimation guarantees under the fatness assumption, improving the prior sample complexity to $O( \log n / ε^2)$ for $\ell_\infty$-recovery, matching the untruncated minimax rate. We further generalize fatness using the notion of influence utilized in the analysis of Boolean functions and provide sufficient conditions for efficient inference. Notably, unlike previous work, our method does not require sampling at arbitrary parameterizations of the model. Lastly, we establish a theoretical lower bound demonstrating the sample complexity exhibits an intrinsic exponential dependence on the width of the model and the minimum distance between elements in the set.

</details>


### [250] [Learning from the Descent Direction: Adaptive Gradient Descent under One-Sided Hölder Regularity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22906)
*Arzu Ahmadova,Ismail Huseynov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对单边Hölder光滑非凸目标的自适应梯度下降方法，通过估计正单边Hölder曲率并结合充分下降保障，实现了比传统Lipschitz梯度方法更优的步长自适应性和收敛保证，在两类基准问题上表现优越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统梯度下降分析通常假设全梯度变分受Lipschitz或Hölder条件控制，但这忽略了梯度变化中不阻碍下降的分量，导致步长选择过于保守。本文旨在利用单边Hölder曲率这一更精细的曲率条件，设计能够自适应局部下降几何的自适应梯度方法。

Method: 提出一种基于正单边Hölder曲率估计的自适应标量步长方法，结合简单的充分下降保障机制。在包含接受更新段的凸区域上的非凸目标下，证明最优迭代驻点界，其收敛速率由Hölder指数决定。

Result: 在二分类基准上，该方法取得了最低的交叉熵、目标值和梯度范数，以及最大的分类间隔；在非凸Hölder回归问题上，取得了最低的最终目标差距和梯度范数。

Conclusion: 单边Hölder曲率是一种有效的自适应步长信号，尤其当全梯度变分被不阻碍下降的方向放大时，该方法相比传统的预定步长递减方案更具优势。

Abstract: We study adaptive gradient descent for continuously differentiable, possibly nonconvex objectives under one-sided Hölder regularity. Unlike classical Hölder- or Lipschitz-gradient assumptions, which control the full gradient variation, our condition bounds only the directional term appearing in the descent inequality. This can allow less conservative step sizes when large gradient changes are orthogonal to, or favorable along, the update direction. We propose an adaptive scalar-step method based on an estimate of positive one-sided Hölder curvature, combined with a simple sufficient-decrease safeguard. For nonconvex objectives on a convex region containing the accepted update segments, we prove an explicit best-iterate stationarity bound with a rate determined by the Hölder exponent. Unlike predetermined diminishing step-size schemes, the method adapts to the local descent geometry. We evaluate the approach on two full-batch benchmarks designed to separate directional curvature from full gradient variation. On a binary classification problem, the method achieves the lowest final cross-entropy, objective value, and gradient norm, together with the largest classification margin among the compared scalar gradient methods. On a nonconvex Hölder regression problem, it attains the lowest final objective gap and gradient norm. These results indicate that one-sided Hölder curvature is an effective adaptive step-size signal when full-gradient variation is inflated by directions that do not hinder descent.

</details>


### [251] [Beyond Directed Acyclic Graphs: Causal Zeros and Causal Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22910)
*Sergei V. Kalinin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对Pearl结构因果模型（SCM）中必须预设因果方向且禁止反馈环的两大限制，提出扩展因果模型：通过引入激活算子将对称物理约束（如理想气体定律）形式化为因果零点，并通过因果微分方程（CDE）将表观瞬时循环解释为时间展开的无环因果过程，给出了扩展do-演算、可识别性条件、反事实语义及开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Pearl的SCM框架要求所有关系必须预先指定为有向因果边，且禁止反馈循环，但这无法处理对称物理约束（如理想气体定律）和表观瞬时循环现象，亟需扩展框架以覆盖这些场景。

Method: 1. 提出扩展因果模型，通过激活算子（activation operator）将对称约束形式化为因果零点（causal zeros），并施加局部可解性和图可接受性条件。2. 针对有限传播状态空间系统，利用因果微分方程（CDE）将瞬态过程视为时间展开的无环因果过程，将因果零点定义为吸引平衡流形的定义函数，并通过吸引子相对干预处理周期和混沌吸引子。

Result: 给出了扩展do-演算、可识别性条件、反事实语义，并提出了若干开放问题。

Conclusion: 通过因果零点和CDE，本文成功将对称约束和反馈循环纳入因果框架，拓展了SCM的表达能力，但仍有若干开放问题待解决。

Abstract: Pearl's structural causal model (SCM) framework, built on directed acyclic graphs (DAGs) and the do-calculus, is the dominant formal language for causal reasoning. Yet it carries two structural restrictions: every relationship must be pre-specified as a directed causal edge, and feedback cycles are forbidden. This paper examines two classes of phenomena that strain these restrictions. First, symmetric physical and economic constraints, the ideal gas law being the canonical case, carry no intrinsic causal direction. Direction emerges only under intervention, and which variable is solved for must be specified as part of the intervention. We formalize such constraints as causal zeros within an Extended Causal Model by adding an activation operator, subject to local solvability and graph-admissibility conditions. Second, for the class of finite-propagation state-space systems considered here, we treat apparent instantaneous cycles as artifacts of suppressed time and ground both causal zeros and feedback in Causal Differential Equations (CDEs). In these, the transient regime is a time-unrolled acyclic causal process, and causal zeros arise as the defining functions of attracting equilibrium manifolds; periodic and chaotic attractors define further regimes of the same dynamics, treated through attractor-relative intervention. We give the extended do-calculus, identifiability conditions, counterfactual semantics, and open problems.

</details>


### [252] [Hidden Boundary Motion in Transformer Optimization: Function-Space Orthogonalization of Affine Weight and Bias Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22927)
*Zhang Gongyue,Sheng Yixuan,Liu donghan,Wang Zhiyong,Ren Weihong,Liu honghai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现神经网络中权重和偏置的优化存在隐藏耦合：当输入均值非零时，权重更新中的样本无关项ΔWμ在功能上与偏置更新不可区分，称为"边界运动"。实验表明这种边界运动主要通过权重矩阵实现，而非显式偏置。提出的SBO-AdamW优化器通过解耦形状和边界分量，将验证准确率从81.68%提升至85.81%，但存在偏置坐标漂移问题，仍需稳定的中心化仿射参数化方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示神经网络优化中权重和偏置之间被忽视的耦合关系，即当输入均值非零时，权重更新中的样本无关部分（边界运动）与偏置更新功能等价，导致优化目标不匹配。

Method: 1）将权重更新分解为形状分量（中心化、样本相关）和边界分量（样本无关）；2）设计SBO-AdamW优化器，分别优化去偏后的权重梯度和偏置梯度，并使用独立的Adam状态；3）在四层Transformer上从头训练验证。

Result: 在IMDb数据集上，边界项g_bμ^T的中位数范数占原始权重梯度范数的0.664；||ΔWμ||/||Δb||中位数达134.7，||ΔWμ||/||Δb+ΔWμ||为0.994。SBO-AdamW将验证准确率从81.68%提升至85.81%，验证选择测试准确率从78.73%提升至82.73%，最佳验证点从3000步提前至800步。

Conclusion: 隐藏的边界运动是重要的优化机制，但目前的补偿方案会导致偏置坐标漂移和边界能量下降，尚未形成通用的最终优化器，需要稳定的中心化仿射参数化作为下一步。

Abstract: Weights and biases are normally optimized as separate parameter tensors, yet they do not represent separate functions when the input to an affine layer has nonzero mean. For an affine map $z=Wx+b$ with input mean $μ$, a weight update contains a sample-independent displacement $ΔWμ$ that is functionally indistinguishable from a bias update. We call this hidden contribution \emph{boundary motion} and decompose each update into a centered, sample-varying \emph{shape} component and a shared \emph{boundary} component. On a four-layer Transformer trained from scratch on IMDb, the bias-like term $g_bμ^\top$ has a median norm equal to 0.664 of the raw weight-gradient norm across affine layers and training checkpoints. More strikingly, the median ratio $\norm{ΔWμ}/\norm{Δb}$ is 134.7, while $\norm{ΔWμ}/\norm{Δb+ΔWμ}$ is 0.994. Thus, under AdamW, the observed boundary motion is almost entirely realized through the weight matrix rather than the explicit bias.
  We implement a diagnostic optimizer, Shape--Boundary Orthogonal AdamW (SBO-AdamW), that optimizes $g_W-g_bμ^\top$ and $g_b$ with independent Adam states and compensates the weight-induced boundary displacement. In a single-seed experiment, SBO-AdamW raises validation accuracy from 81.68\% to 85.81\% and validation-selected test accuracy from 78.73\% to 82.73\%, with the best validation checkpoint occurring at step 800 instead of step 3000. However, the moving-batch-center compensation produces severe bias-coordinate drift and strongly reduces boundary energy. The present evidence therefore supports hidden boundary motion as an important optimization mechanism, but it does not yet establish a final general-purpose optimizer. A stable centered-affine parameterization is identified as the required next step.

</details>


### [253] [Distribution-Specific Curvature Control with Finite-Sample Guarantees for Open-Weight Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22929)
*Domenic Rosati,Ali Dadsetan,Hong Huang,Xijie Zeng,Hassan Chowdhry,Subhabrata Majumdar,Hassan Sajjad,Frank Rudzicz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HarmAlign方法，通过函数保持的谱变形在估计的对比激活子空间上操作，在保持良性任务可训练性的同时阻止有害微调，并提供了理论保证和实证验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全防护方法（如谱变形）全局曲率膨胀会同时阻碍有害和良性适应，缺乏既能防御有害微调又不影响良性任务适应性的有效方法。

Method: HarmAlign：在估计的对比激活子空间上进行函数保持的谱变形，推导子空间能量和局部有害分布曲率下界的有限样本界，利用稳定-进展二分法将认证曲率转化为条件收敛率控制。

Result: 在固定架构、有限预算的一阶威胁模型下，HarmAlign在危险知识重新学习和有害辅助微调设置中，有效阻止了直接微调和三种数据或目标自适应攻击，同时良性任务保持可训练性。

Conclusion: HarmAlign能够在防御有害微调的同时保持良性任务的可适应性，防御效果在多种一阶优化器变体、分布外有害微调、意外安全性退化和突发错位场景中均保持有效。

Abstract: A short fine-tuning run can undo the safety guards of an open-weight model---retraining a refusal-trained assistant to aid weapons development or produce hate speech. Preventing such harmful fine-tuning while retaining benign adaptability remains difficult: the only prior method with an explicit curvature certificate, spectral deformation, inflates curvature globally and thereby obstructs benign adaptation along with harmful adaptation. We propose HarmAlign, which applies function-preserving spectral deformation along a estimated contrastive activation subspace. We derive finite-sample bounds for the estimated subspace energy and the resulting local harmful-distribution curvature lower bound. A stability--progress dichotomy for constant-step gradient descent turns the certified curvature into conditional convergence-rate control. Empirically, within a fixed-architecture, finite-budget first-order threat model, HarmAlign blocks direct fine-tuning and three data- or objective-adaptive attacks across a hazardous-knowledge relearning setting and a harmful-assistance fine-tuning setting, while the protected benign tasks remain trainable. The block persists across the tested first-order optimizer variants over every attack checkpoint, and under out-of-distribution harmful fine-tuning, and it extends to important cases in our threat model: accidental safety degradation and emergent misalignment.

</details>


### [254] [Spectral-Aware Analytic Class-Incremental Learning for Long-Tailed Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22931)
*Quyen Tran,Hai Nguyen,Quan Dao,Zhuowei Li,Nam Le,Trung Le,Dimitris Metaxas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出几何-谱整流（GSR）方法，通过各向异性谱滤波解决解析持续学习中长尾分布导致的谱坍缩问题，在类别增量学习场景中达到新最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有解析持续学习（ACL）方法在长尾分布的类增量学习场景中性能显著下降，原因是自相关矩阵的病态条件被类别不平衡加剧，导致尾部类别发生严重谱坍缩，而标准岭回归的各向同性正则化无法有效解决这一问题。

Method: 提出几何-谱整流（GSR）框架，通过构造结构化、数据依赖的谱扰动矩阵Δ，对各向异性谱滤波，选择性地膨胀尾部类别的坍缩特征值，改善Gram矩阵的稳定秩。

Result: 广泛实验表明GSR在解析类增量学习中达到新的最优性能，在长尾设置下实现了计算效率与鲁棒泛化之间的优越权衡。

Conclusion: GSR作为一种理论驱动的各向异性谱正则化方法，有效解决了长尾分布下解析持续学习的谱坍缩问题，为持续学习提供了新的理论视角和实用方案。

Abstract: Analytic Continual Learning (ACL) offers a computationally efficient alternative to gradient-based approaches. Recent ACL methods are based on Recursive Least Squares (RLS) and have achieved the state-of-the-art results compared to other alternatives. However, they falter significantly in Class-Incremental Learning scenarios characterized by Long-Tailed distributions. While the ill-conditioning of the autocorrelation (Gram) matrix is a known limitation of RLS, we demonstrate that class imbalance exacerbates this issue into a distinct spectral pathology: "tail" classes suffer from severe spectral collapse, rendering their subspaces numerically indistinguishable from noise. Standard Ridge Regression ($L_2$) fails to address this effectively as it applies isotropic regularization - a uniform penalty that is insufficient to stabilize the tail without over-shrinking the head. To address this, we propose Geometry-Spectral Rectification (GSR), a theoretically grounded framework that treats long-tailed learning as a spectral regularization problem. Unlike standard isotropic regularization (Ridge) which uniformly penalizes all eigenvalues, GSR acts as an anisotropic spectral filter, selectively inflating the collapsed eigenvalues of tail classes. We construct a structured, data-dependent spectral perturbation matrix $Δ$ that selectively inflates collapsed tail eigen-directions of the Gram matrix. Theoretical analysis proves that GSR guarantees an improved stable rank for the Gram matrix, ensuring numerical stability. Extensive experiments show that GSR establishes a new state-of-the-art for analytic CIL, offering a superior trade-off between computational efficiency and robust generalization in long-tailed settings.

</details>


### [255] [Discrepancy-Rounded Fair Bandits with Static and Time-Varying Exposure Floors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22935)
*Ibne Farabi Shihab,Joyanta Jyoti Mondal,Anuj Sharma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究随机bandit中带有精确曝光下限的问题，提出基于Beck-Fiala舍入的块级模型与BDQ-UCB等算法，实现确定性满足曝光约束，遗憾界由非强制预算R而非时间T主导，并在重叠组约束下达到最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在推荐系统、内容策展和资源分配中，每个提供方或群体必须在每个时间段内获得最低曝光保证，而聚合层面的保证不足。现有方法难以同时满足精确曝光下限和低遗憾。

Method: 将问题建模为舍入问题：分数公平调度被实现为整数拉动，曝光误差即为差异性向量。提出块级模型（时间变化下限），BDQ-UCB算法确定性满足块级下限，MOSS残差变体与kl-UCB$^{++}$残差规则分别达到渐近最优和实例最优。对重叠组约束，采用Beck-Fiala零空间舍入，每个组约束在块预算内满足且违反低于臂度数t。

Result: BDQ-UCB遗憾界O(√(KR log(KT)))，MOSS变体O(√(KR))，匹配下界Θ(√(KR))。重叠组约束下违反度低于臂度数t。对学习到的组计划，不相交系统达到Θ̃(√(KT))，重叠系统在初始覆盖松弛条件下达到条件性Õ(√(KT))。

Conclusion: 所提方法在不依赖惩罚调参的情况下实现了精确可行性，遗憾性能与调参后的Lagrangian基线相当，为曝光约束下的bandit问题提供了完整理论框架。

Abstract: Minimum-exposure constraints arise in recommendation, content curation, and regulated allocation when each provider, arm, or group must receive guaranteed exposure inside a period rather than only in aggregate.
